####################################################################################################
FILE: application\Launcher.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Launcher.py — preferred entry-point for the Compliance Chatbot.

Starts both the FastAPI backend (application.main:app) and the standalone
Python UI proxy server (run_ui.py) as sub-processes.

A watchdog loop monitors both processes and reports if either exits.

Usage:
    python application/Launcher.py          # run from anywhere
    python launcher.py                      # thin root-level wrapper

Environment variables (all optional — defaults shown):
    PORT           FastAPI backend port   (default: 8001, auto-increments if busy)
    CDSW_APP_PORT  Standalone UI port     (default: 8100, auto-increments if busy)
    CB_BACKEND_URL Overrides backend URL injected into the UI (set automatically)
"""

import os
import signal
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

_IS_WINDOWS = sys.platform == "win32"

# On Linux/cloud (CDSW/CML) the backend must bind on all interfaces so the UI
# proxy and external traffic can reach it.  On Windows loopback is sufficient.
_BIND_HOST = "127.0.0.1" if _IS_WINDOWS else "0.0.0.0"

# Project root is one level up from application/.
_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


# ── Port helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _is_port_free(port: int) -> bool:
    """Return True when nothing is listening on <_BIND_HOST>:<port>."""
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.settimeout(0.2)
        return s.connect_ex((_BIND_HOST, port)) != 0


def _find_free_port(preferred: int, max_attempts: int = 20) -> int:
    """
    Return *preferred* if it is free, otherwise scan upward until a free port
    is found (up to *max_attempts* ports above *preferred*).

    Raises RuntimeError when no free port is found in range.
    """
    for candidate in range(preferred, preferred + max_attempts):
        if _is_port_free(candidate):
            return candidate
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"No free port found in range [{preferred}, {preferred + max_attempts}). "
        "Free a port or set PORT / CDSW_APP_PORT explicitly."
    )


# ── Backend readiness poll ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _wait_for_backend(url: str, timeout: int = 30, interval: float = 0.5) -> bool:
    """
    Poll GET <url>/health until it returns HTTP 200 or *timeout* seconds elapse.

    Returns True when the backend is ready, False on timeout.
    """
    deadline = time.time() + timeout
    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(f"{url}/health", timeout=2) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    return True
        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError):
            pass
        time.sleep(interval)
    return False


# ── Main launcher ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def launch_compliance_bot() -> None:
    """Start the backend and UI sub-processes and monitor them."""
    ui_script = _PROJECT_ROOT / "application" / "run_ui.py"

    if not ui_script.exists():
        print(f" X ERROR: UI script not found at {ui_script}")
        sys.exit(1)

    # Resolve ports — auto-find free ones if the preferred ports are occupied.
    preferred_backend = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8001"))
    preferred_ui      = int(os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", "8100"))

    print("\n Checking environment...")

    try:
        backend_port = _find_free_port(preferred_backend)
        if backend_port != preferred_backend:
            print(f" ! Port {preferred_backend} busy — using {backend_port} for backend.")
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        print(f" X {exc}")
        sys.exit(1)

    try:
        ui_port = _find_free_port(preferred_ui)
        if ui_port != preferred_ui:
            print(f" ! Port {preferred_ui} busy — using {ui_port} for UI.")
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        print(f" X {exc}")
        sys.exit(1)

    # On Linux bind to all interfaces; health-check and UI still reach it via
    # loopback.  On Windows keep loopback for simplicity.
    backend_url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{backend_port}"

    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["CB_BACKEND_URL"] = backend_url
    env["CDSW_APP_PORT"]  = str(ui_port)

    print("Starting Compliance Bot Suite...")

    backend_proc = None
    ui_proc      = None

    # On Windows, CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP isolates Ctrl+C so it does not
    # propagate to child processes; the launcher handles shutdown itself.
    _popen_kwargs: dict = {}
    if _IS_WINDOWS:
        _popen_kwargs["creationflags"] = subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP

    try:
        # ── 1. Start FastAPI backend ──────────────────────────────────────────
        print(f" > Launching Backend (FastAPI) on port {backend_port}...")
        backend_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [
                sys.executable, "-m", "uvicorn", "application.main:app",
                "--host", _BIND_HOST,
                "--port", str(backend_port),
            ],
            env=env,
            cwd=str(_PROJECT_ROOT),
            **_popen_kwargs,
        )

        # ── 2. Wait until backend is actually ready (health check) ────────────
        print(f" > Waiting for backend to be ready at {backend_url}/health ...")
        ready = _wait_for_backend(backend_url, timeout=60)
        if not ready:
            # Check if process already died
            if backend_proc.poll() is not None:
                print(f"\n X Backend exited with code {backend_proc.returncode} before becoming ready.")
            else:
                print("\n X Backend did not respond within 60 seconds.")
                backend_proc.terminate()
            sys.exit(1)

        print(f" > Backend is ready.")

        # ── 3. Start standalone UI proxy server ───────────────────────────────
        print(f" > Launching UI Server on port {ui_port}...")
        ui_proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [sys.executable, str(ui_script)],
            env=env,
            cwd=str(_PROJECT_ROOT),
            **_popen_kwargs,
        )

        print()
        print("=" * 62)
        print(" SYSTEMS STARTED")
        print(f"   Backend  (FastAPI):  {backend_url}")
        print(f"   Frontend (UI proxy): http://127.0.0.1:{ui_port}")
        print("=" * 62)
        print(" Press Ctrl+C to stop both servers.\n")

        # ── 4. Watchdog loop ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)

            rc = backend_proc.poll()
            if rc is not None:
                print(f"\n X BACKEND EXITED (exit code {rc}). Check logs above.")
                break

            rc = ui_proc.poll()
            if rc is not None:
                print(f"\n X UI SERVER EXITED (exit code {rc}). Check logs above.")
                break

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n Shutting down servers...")
    finally:
        # Windows: send CTRL_BREAK to the process group for a graceful shutdown.
        # Linux/macOS: terminate() sends SIGTERM which is the correct signal.
        for proc in (backend_proc, ui_proc):
            if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
                try:
                    if _IS_WINDOWS:
                        proc.send_signal(signal.CTRL_BREAK_EVENT)
                    else:
                        proc.terminate()
                except OSError:
                    pass
        print(" All processes stopped.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    launch_compliance_bot()



####################################################################################################
FILE: application\main.py
####################################################################################################

"""
application/main.py — FastAPI backend application for the Compliance Chatbot v2.

This is the ASGI application that uvicorn loads:

    uvicorn application.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000

Wires together:
  - LangGraph NL2SQL agentic pipeline  (genai/nl2sql_agent/)
  - ComplianceProcessor                (genai/core/compliance_processor.py)
  - Static assets and CORS middleware

Endpoints:
  GET  /health                        — liveness probe
  POST /api/v1/config/llm             — configure & test LLM connection
  POST /api/v1/compliance/chat        — simplified NL-to-SQL chat response
  POST /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate — full agentic orchestration response
  GET  /                              — serve index.html SPA shell
"""

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncGenerator

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from genai.core.exceptions import ComplianceBotError
from genai.core.model import ModelLoaderService
from genai.core.processor import ComplianceProcessor
from genai.core.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    HealthResponse,
    LLMConnectionRequest,
    LLMConnectionResponse,
)
from genai.nl2sql_agent.graph import build_nl2sql_graph
from genai.utils.config import ModelSettings, get_settings
from genai.utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.application")

_model_service: ModelLoaderService | None = None
_compliance_processor: ComplianceProcessor | None = None

# Project root — two levels above this file  (application/main.py → project root).
_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def _build_processor(model_settings: ModelSettings) -> tuple[ModelLoaderService, ComplianceProcessor]:
    """
    Construct a ``ModelLoaderService``, build the LangGraph pipeline, and
    return both the service and the compliance processor.
    """
    model_service = ModelLoaderService(settings=model_settings)
    model_service.load()

    db_path = get_settings().database.db_path
    compiled_graph = build_nl2sql_graph(
        model_service=model_service,
        db_path=db_path,
    )
    processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=compiled_graph)
    return model_service, processor


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None, None]:
    """FastAPI lifespan handler — startup / shutdown."""
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement
    settings = get_settings()
    LOGGER.info("Starting %s ...", settings.application_name)

    try:
        _model_service, _compliance_processor = _build_processor(settings.model)
    except ComplianceBotError as exc:
        LOGGER.error("Model loading failed — API will return 503 on inference: %s", exc)

    yield

    LOGGER.info("Shutting down %s ...", settings.application_name)
    if _model_service and _model_service.is_loaded():
        _model_service.unload()


settings = get_settings()
app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.application_name,
    description=(
        "LangGraph-based agentic NL-to-SQL compliance query engine for banking "
        "term-deposit products.  Six specialised agents collaborate to analyse "
        "intent, retrieve schema, generate, review, validate, and execute SQL queries."
    ),
    version="2.0.0",
    docs_url="/docs",
    redoc_url="/redoc",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Mount static assets from the project-root assets/ directory.
_assets_dir = _PROJECT_ROOT / "assets"
if _assets_dir.exists():
    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=str(_assets_dir)), name="assets")


@app.exception_handler(ComplianceBotError)
async def compliance_bot_exception_handler(
    _request: Request,
    exc: ComplianceBotError,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """Map domain errors to a structured 500 JSON response."""
    LOGGER.error("Domain error: %s", exc.message)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        content={"error": exc.message},
    )


@app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
async def index() -> FileResponse:
    """Serve the single-page application shell."""
    # Prefer the HTML file inside extra/ (original location), fall back to root.
    index_path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "extra" / "index.html"
    if not index_path.exists():
        index_path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "index.html"
    return FileResponse(str(index_path))


@app.get(
    "/health",
    response_model=HealthResponse,
    summary="Liveness probe",
    tags=["Infrastructure"],
)
async def health_check() -> HealthResponse:
    """Return service health status."""
    return HealthResponse(status="ok", version="2.0.0")


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/config/llm",
    response_model=LLMConnectionResponse,
    tags=["Configuration"],
    summary="Configure and test LLM connection",
)
async def configure_llm(request: LLMConnectionRequest) -> LLMConnectionResponse:
    """Reconfigure the active LLM provider and rebuild the LangGraph pipeline."""
    global _model_service, _compliance_processor  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    base_settings = get_settings().model.model_copy(deep=True)
    candidate_settings = base_settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "provider": request.provider,
            "model_name": request.model_name or base_settings.model_name,
            "dlh_base_url": request.dlh_base_url or base_settings.dlh_base_url,
            "dlh_api_key": request.dlh_api_key or base_settings.dlh_api_key,
            "dlh_verify_ssl": request.dlh_verify_ssl,
            "ica_base_url": request.ica_base_url or base_settings.ica_base_url,
            "ica_api_key": request.ica_api_key or base_settings.ica_api_key,
            "ica_namespace": request.ica_namespace or base_settings.ica_namespace,
            "ica_send_temperature": request.ica_send_temperature,
        }
    )

    model_service, processor = _build_processor(candidate_settings)
    model_service.test_connection()
    _model_service = model_service
    _compliance_processor = processor

    return LLMConnectionResponse(
        success=True,
        provider=candidate_settings.provider,
        message="LLM connection successful. LangGraph pipeline is ready.",
    )


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/chat",
    response_model=ComplianceChatResponse,
    summary="Natural-language compliance query (simplified response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_chat(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceChatResponse:
    """Run the NL2SQL agentic pipeline and return a simplified response."""
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    LOGGER.info("Received chat request — question_id=%s", request.question_id)
    return _compliance_processor.process_query(request)


@app.post(
    "/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
    response_model=ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    summary="Agentic NL2SQL compliance query (full orchestration response)",
    tags=["Compliance"],
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
async def compliance_orchestrate(request: ComplianceChatRequest) -> ComplianceOrchestrationResponse:
    """Run the full LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline and return agent-level detail."""
    if _compliance_processor is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail="Compliance processor is not initialised. Please retry in a few seconds.",
        )
    return _compliance_processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import uvicorn

    _settings = get_settings()
    uvicorn.run(
        "application.main:app",
        host=_settings.host,
        port=_settings.port,
        reload=_settings.debug,
        log_level=_settings.log_level.lower(),
    )



####################################################################################################
FILE: application\run_ui.py
####################################################################################################

"""
run_ui.py — Self-contained UI server for Compliance Bot (LangGraph).

Run:
    python run_ui.py

What it does:
  • Embeds the full single-page app as a Python string (no separate index.html needed).
  • Starts Python's built-in HTTP server on port 5000 (override: CB_UI_PORT env var).
  • Proxies every /api/v1/... request to the FastAPI backend
    (default http://0.0.0.0:8066 — override: CB_BACKEND_URL env var).
  • Renders live LangGraph six-node stage traces returned by /api/v1/compliance/orchestrate.
  • Opens the browser automatically.

No extra dependencies beyond the Python standard library.
"""

import json
import os
import sys
import threading
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import webbrowser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

# Configuration
#UI_PORT: int = int(os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT","5001")))
UI_PORT: int = int(os.environ.get("CDSW_APP_PORT", os.getenv("CB_UI_PORT", "5001")))
BACKEND_URL: str = os.getenv("CB_BACKEND_URL", "http://0.0.0.0:8066").rstrip("/")

# Fonts directory — always resolved relative to this file, regardless of cwd.
# Handles both: python application/run_ui.py  AND  python run_ui.py (from project root)
_THIS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
_FONTS_DIR = _THIS_DIR / "fonts" if (_THIS_DIR / "fonts").is_dir() else _THIS_DIR / "application" / "fonts"

HTML = r"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>Compliance Bot</title>
  <style>
    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-Thin.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 100;
      font-style: normal;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-ThinItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 100;
      font-style: italic;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-ExtraLight.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 200;
      font-style: normal;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-ExtraLightItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 200;
      font-style: italic;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-Light.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 300;
      font-style: normal;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-LightItalic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 300;
      font-style: italic;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-Regular.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 400;
      font-style: normal;
    }

    @font-face {
      font-family: "Urbanist";
      src: url("/fonts/Urbanist-Italic.ttf") format("truetype");
      font-weight: 400;

                <input id="dlhApiKey" value="EMPTY" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div id="icaFields" class="hidden">
              <div class="field">
                <label for="icaBaseUrl">ICA Base URL</label>
                <input id="icaBaseUrl" value="https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com" />
              </div>
              <div class="field">
                <label for="icaApiKey">ICA API Key</label>
                <input id="icaApiKey" />
              </div>
              <div class="field">
                <label for="icaNamespace">ICA Namespace</label>
                <select id="icaNamespace">
                  <option value="chat-models">chat-models</option>
                  <option value="agents">agents</option>
                  <option value="assistants">assistants</option>
                  <option value="digital-workforce">digital-workforce</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="actions">
              <button class="primary" id="connectBtn">Test & Save</button>
              <button class="secondary" id="loadConfigBtn">Load Saved Config</button>
            </div>
            <div id="statusBox" class="status"></div>
          </section>

          <section class="panel">
            <h3>Saved Configuration</h3>
            <p class="panel-subtitle">Settings are stored locally so they remain available after refresh.</p>
            <div class="code-block" id="savedConfigPreview">No saved configuration yet.</div>
          </section>
        </section>

        <section class="page chat-page" data-page="chat">
          <section class="panel">
            <h3>Conversational Chat</h3>
            <p class="panel-subtitle">Ask natural-language compliance questions — six LangGraph nodes: Intent → Schema → SQL Writer (LLM) → SQL Review → SQL Validator → Executor.</p>

            <div class="stages" id="stageTrail">
              <div class="stage" title="Node 1 (intent.py) — 4 checks: empty, disallowed action word, too short, vague/ambiguous">Intent Checker</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 2 (schema.py) — auto-discovers databases; scores by keyword overlap; builds live schema context">Schema Checker</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 3 (sql_writer_agent.py) — only LLM call; few-shot prompt + live schema; extracts SQL from tags">SQL Writer</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 4 (sql_review.py) — blocks DML/DDL; requires SELECT + FROM; checks table existence; up to 3 retries">SQL Review</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 4.5 (sql_validator.py) — sqlglot AST parse; SELECT-only gate; SQLite dialect canonicalisation">SQL Validator</div>
              <div class="stage" title="Node 5 (executor.py) — BEGIN DEFERRED; fetchmany(500); always rollback; builds result summary">Executor</div>
            </div>

            <div class="chat-window" id="chatWindow"></div>

            <div class="composer">
              <div class="field">
                <label for="questionInput">Message</label>
                <textarea id="questionInput" placeholder="Example: How many person from Mumbai circle? Press Enter to send, Shift+Enter for a new line."></textarea>
                <div class="hint">Press Enter to send. Use Shift + Enter to insert a new line.</div>
              </div>
              <div class="composer-buttons">
                <button class="secondary" id="copyChatBtn">Copy Chat</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="exportChatBtn">Export Chat</button>
                <button class="secondary" id="clearBtn">New Chat</button>
                <span class="send-btn-wrap">
                  <button class="primary" id="sendBtn">Send</button>
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        </section>

      </section>
    </main>
  </div>

  <script>
    const appShell = document.getElementById('appShell');
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const resizeHandle = document.getElementById('resizeHandle');
    const collapseBtn = document.getElementById('collapseBtn');
    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
    const pageTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
    const pageSubtitle = document.getElementById('pageSubtitle');
    const navItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item'));
    const pages = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.page'));
    const provider = document.getElementById('provider');
    const dlhFields = document.getElementById('dlhFields');
    const icaFields = document.getElementById('icaFields');
    const statusBox = document.getElementById('statusBox');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');
    const questionInput = document.getElementById('questionInput');
    const savedConfigPreview = document.getElementById('savedConfigPreview');

    const STORAGE_KEYS = {
      sidebarWidth: 'cb_sidebar_width',
      sidebarCollapsed: 'cb_sidebar_collapsed',
      activePage: 'cb_active_page',
      llmConfig: 'cb_llm_config'
    };

    const PAGE_META = {
      'llm-config': { title: 'LLM Configuration', subtitle: 'Configure the LLM provider — the LangGraph pipeline rebuilds automatically on save.' },
      chat: { title: 'Chat', subtitle: 'Six LangGraph nodes: Intent Checker → Schema Checker → SQL Writer (LLM) → SQL Review → SQL Validator → Executor.' }
    };

    // Maps a LangGraph stage string prefix to the display name of the stage pill.
    // Prefixes must match the strings emitted by each agent node exactly:
    //   intent_checker.py    → "intent_checker:…"
    //   schema_checker.py    → "schema_checker:…"
    //   sql_writer_agent.py  → "sql_writer_agent:…"
    //   sql_reviewer.py      → "sql_reviewer:…"
    //   sql_validator.py     → "sql_validator:…"
    //   executor_agent.py    → "executor_agent:…"
    const STAGE_PREFIX_MAP = [
      { prefix: 'intent_checker',   label: 'Intent Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'schema_checker',   label: 'Schema Checker'  },
      { prefix: 'sql_writer_agent', label: 'SQL Writer'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_reviewer',     label: 'SQL Review'      },
      { prefix: 'sql_validator',    label: 'SQL Validator'   },
      { prefix: 'executor_agent',   label: 'Executor'        },
      { prefix: 'clarify_node',     label: 'Clarification'   },
    ];

    /**
     * Highlight the stage pills that appear in the live LangGraph stage trace.
     * Each pill's data-prefix attribute is compared against every stage string
     * returned by the orchestrate endpoint; a match lights the pill up in accent blue.
     */
    function updateStagePills(stages) {
      const trail = document.getElementById('stageTrail');
      if (!trail) return;
      const pills = trail.querySelectorAll('.stage');
      pills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.style.background = '';
        pill.style.color = '';
        pill.style.borderColor = '';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '';
      });
      STAGE_PREFIX_MAP.forEach(({ prefix, label }, idx) => {
        const matched = stages.some(s => s.startsWith(prefix));
        if (!matched) return;
        const pill = pills[idx];
        if (!pill) return;
        pill.style.background = '#2563eb';
        pill.style.color = '#ffffff';
        pill.style.borderColor = '#1d4ed8';
        pill.style.fontWeight = '600';
      });
    }

    function setSidebarWidth(width) {
      const min = 220;
      const max = 420;
      const clamped = Math.min(max, Math.max(min, width));
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-width', `${clamped}px`);
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth, String(clamped));
    }

    function setCollapsed(collapsed) {
      appShell.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed', collapsed);
      collapseBtn.textContent = collapsed ? '▶' : '◀';
      collapseBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', collapsed ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar');
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed, String(collapsed));
    }

    function toggleProviderFields() {
      dlhFields.classList.toggle('hidden', provider.value !== 'dlh');
      icaFields.classList.toggle('hidden', provider.value !== 'ica');
    }

    function saveConfigLocally(config) {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig, JSON.stringify(config));
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    }

    function loadConfigLocally() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.llmConfig);
      if (!saved) {
        savedConfigPreview.textContent = 'No saved configuration yet.';
        return null;
      }
      const config = JSON.parse(saved);
      provider.value = config.provider || 'dlh';
      document.getElementById('modelName').value = config.model_name || 'google/gemma-4-31b-it';
      document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value = config.dlh_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value = config.dlh_api_key || '';
      document.getElementById('icaBaseUrl').value = config.ica_base_url || '';
      document.getElementById('icaApiKey').value = config.ica_api_key || '';
      document.getElementById('icaNamespace').value = config.ica_namespace || 'chat-models';
      toggleProviderFields();
      savedConfigPreview.textContent = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
      return config;
    }

    function renderMarkdown(text) {
      const escaped = text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return escaped
        .replace(/```([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre class="code-block">$1</pre>')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    function addMessage(text, role, meta, persist = true) {
      const msg = document.createElement('div');
      msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
      msg.innerHTML = role === 'bot' ? renderMarkdown(text) : text;
      chatWindow.appendChild(msg);

      if (meta) {
        const metaBox = document.createElement('div');
        metaBox.className = 'meta markdown';
        metaBox.innerHTML = meta;
        chatWindow.appendChild(metaBox);
      }

      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;

      if (persist) {
        chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
      }
    }

    function clearChat() {
      chatWindow.innerHTML = '';
      delete chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation;
      updateStagePills([]); // reset all pills to default (unlit) state
    }

    function setActivePage(pageId) {
      navItems.forEach((item) => item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === pageId));
      pages.forEach((page) => page.classList.toggle('active', page.dataset.page === pageId));
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage, pageId);
      pageTitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].title;
      pageSubtitle.textContent = PAGE_META[pageId].subtitle;
      if (window.innerWidth <= 920) {
        appShell.classList.remove('mobile-sidebar-open');
      }
    }

    async function connectProvider() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('connectBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;

      // Show spinning dots while connecting
      statusBox.className = 'status testing';
      statusBox.innerHTML =
        '<div class="status-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>' +
        '<span>Testing connection\u2026</span>';

      const payload = {
        provider: provider.value,
        model_name: document.getElementById('modelName').value,
        dlh_base_url: document.getElementById('dlhBaseUrl').value,
        dlh_api_key: document.getElementById('dlhApiKey').value,
        ica_base_url: document.getElementById('icaBaseUrl').value,
        ica_api_key: document.getElementById('icaApiKey').value,
        ica_namespace: document.getElementById('icaNamespace').value
      };

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/config/llm', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Connection failed');
        statusBox.className = 'status success';
        statusBox.textContent = data.message;
        saveConfigLocally(payload);
        addMessage(`Connected to ${data.provider}. LangGraph pipeline is ready.`, 'bot');
        checkBackendHealth();
        setActivePage('chat');
      } catch (error) {
        statusBox.className = 'status error';
        statusBox.textContent = error.message;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    function showTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'typing-indicator';
      el.id = 'typingIndicator';
      el.innerHTML = '<span></span><span></span><span></span>';
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
    }

    function hideTypingIndicator() {
      const el = document.getElementById('typingIndicator');
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    // Render text as markdown (bold + code + pre + newlines).
    function formatNoticeText(text) {
      return text
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>')
        .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>')
        .replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    }

    // Append a styled notice card (blocked = red, clarify = blue) to the chat.
    function addNotice(text, type) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = `msg-notice ${type}`;
      el.innerHTML = formatNoticeText(text);
      chatWindow.appendChild(el);
      chatWindow.scrollTop = chatWindow.scrollHeight;
      chatWindow.dataset.hasConversation = 'true';
    }

    async function sendMessage() {
      const question = questionInput.value.trim();
      if (!question) return;

      const sendBtn = document.getElementById('sendBtn');
      addMessage(question, 'user');
      questionInput.value = '';
      sendBtn.disabled = true;
      sendBtn.textContent = 'Sending\u2026';
      showTypingIndicator();

      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ question })
        });
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || data.detail || 'Chat failed');

        // Update stage pills with live LangGraph trace.
        updateStagePills(data.stages || []);

        const stageTrace = (data.stages || []).join(' → ');
        const stageHtml = `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#57606a;margin-bottom:10px;word-break:break-all">${stageTrace || '—'}</div>`;

        // ── Clarification requested (vague or ambiguous question) ──────────
        if (data.needs_clarification) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.clarification_question, 'clarify');
          return;
        }

        // ── Pipeline error (blocked keyword, max retries, etc.) ────────────
        if (data.error) {
          hideTypingIndicator();
          addNotice(data.error, 'blocked');
          return;
        }

        // ── Successful query result ────────────────────────────────────────
        const rowCount = Array.isArray(data.df_output) ? data.df_output.length : 0;
        const summary  = data.result_summary || `${rowCount} row(s) returned.`;
        const resultsJson = JSON.stringify(data.df_output || [], null, 2);
        const encodedData = encodeURIComponent(resultsJson);
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addMessage(
          'Query completed successfully.',
          'bot',
          `<strong>Agent Trace</strong>${stageHtml}` +
          `<strong>Summary</strong><br>${summary.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>')}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>SQL</strong><pre class="code-block">${(data.sql_query || 'N/A').replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</pre>` +
          `<br><strong>Rows returned:</strong> ${rowCount}<br><br>` +
          `<strong>Results</strong><pre class="code-block" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">${resultsJson}</pre>` +
          `<button class="csv-download-btn" data-csv="${encodedData}" onclick="downloadCsv(this)">&#8595; Download as CSV</button>`
        );
      } catch (error) {
        hideTypingIndicator();
        addNotice('An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.', 'blocked');
      } finally {
        sendBtn.disabled = false;
        sendBtn.textContent = 'Send';
      }
    }

    function downloadCsv(btn) {
      let rows;
      try { rows = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(btn.dataset.csv)); } catch (_) { return; }
      if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { alert('No data to download.'); return; }
      const headers = Object.keys(rows[0]);
      const escape  = v => {
        const s = v === null || v === undefined ? '' : String(v);
        return s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n')
          ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
      };
      const csvContent = [
        headers.map(escape).join(','),
        ...rows.map(r => headers.map(h => escape(r[h])).join(','))
      ].join('\r\n');
      const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
      const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a    = document.createElement('a');
      a.href     = url;
      a.download = `results_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/[:T]/g,'-')}.csv`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    function getConversationEntries() {
      return Array.from(chatWindow.querySelectorAll('.msg, .meta'));
    }

    function copyChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(transcript || 'No conversation available.');
    }

    function exportChat() {
      const transcript = getConversationEntries()
        .map((entry) => entry.innerText.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      const blob = new Blob([transcript || 'No conversation available.'], { type: 'text/plain' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'conversation.txt';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    navItems.forEach((item) => {
      item.addEventListener('click', () => setActivePage(item.dataset.page));
      item.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
          event.preventDefault();
          setActivePage(item.dataset.page);
        }
      });
    });

    provider.addEventListener('change', toggleProviderFields);
    document.getElementById('connectBtn').addEventListener('click', connectProvider);
    document.getElementById('loadConfigBtn').addEventListener('click', loadConfigLocally);
    document.getElementById('sendBtn').addEventListener('click', sendMessage);
    document.getElementById('clearBtn').addEventListener('click', clearChat);
    document.getElementById('copyChatBtn').addEventListener('click', copyChat);
    document.getElementById('exportChatBtn').addEventListener('click', exportChat);

    questionInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
        event.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setCollapsed(!appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed'));
    });

    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      appShell.classList.toggle('mobile-sidebar-open');
    });

    let resizing = false;
    resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
      if (appShell.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') || window.innerWidth <= 920) return;
      resizing = true;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
    });

    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      setSidebarWidth(event.clientX);
    });

    document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
      if (!resizing) return;
      resizing = false;
      document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
    });

    // ── Backend health polling ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    const backendStatus = document.getElementById('backendStatus');
    const backendLabel  = document.getElementById('backendLabel');

    async function checkBackendHealth() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/health', { method: 'GET', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(4000) });
        if (res.ok) {
          backendStatus.className = 'backend-status alive';
          backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Active';
        } else {
          throw new Error('non-ok');
        }
      } catch (_) {
        backendStatus.className = 'backend-status dead';
        backendLabel.textContent = 'Backend Offline';
      }
    }

    checkBackendHealth();
    setInterval(checkBackendHealth, 8000);
    // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const savedWidth = Number(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarWidth) || '280');
    setSidebarWidth(savedWidth);
    setCollapsed(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.sidebarCollapsed) === 'true');
    const savedPage = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.activePage) || 'llm-config';
    setActivePage(savedPage);
    loadConfigLocally();
    toggleProviderFields();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Serve the UI and proxy /api/v1/* to the FastAPI backend."""

    # Silence the default per-request access log lines.
    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        pass

    def _send(self, code: int, content_type: str, body: bytes) -> None:
        try:
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            # Client closed the connection before the response was sent
            # (e.g. browser tab closed, LLM took too long).  Nothing to do.
            pass

    # Serve the HTML page, static assets and proxy GET API calls.
    def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]

        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
            body = HTML.encode("utf-8")
            self._send(200, "text/html; charset=utf-8", body)
            return

        # Serve font files from application/fonts/
        if path.startswith("/fonts/"):
            file_path = (_FONTS_DIR / path[len("/fonts/"):]).resolve()
            # Prevent path traversal outside fonts dir
            if not str(file_path).startswith(str(_FONTS_DIR)):
                self._send(403, "text/plain", b"Forbidden")
                return
            if file_path.is_file():
                data = file_path.read_bytes()
                ct = "font/truetype" if file_path.suffix in (".ttf", ".otf") else "application/octet-stream"
                self._send(200, ct, data)
            else:
                print(f"[font] 404 — file not found: {file_path}")
                self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        # Proxy GET API calls and the backend health probe
        if path.startswith("/api/") or path == "/health":
            self._proxy_get(path)
            return

        self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")

    def _proxy_get(self, path: str) -> None:
        try:
            url = BACKEND_URL + path
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client already disconnected — nothing to send
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)

    # Proxy all POST /api/* requests directly to the FastAPI backend.
    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
        path = self.path.split("?")[0]
        if not path.startswith("/api/"):
            self._send(404, "text/plain", b"Not found")
            return

        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        raw_body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""

        target_url = BACKEND_URL + path
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            target_url,
            data=raw_body,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
            method="POST",
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                body = resp.read()
                self._send(resp.status, resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionAbortedError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass  # client disconnected before response arrived — nothing to send
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            body = exc.read()
            self._send(exc.code, exc.headers.get("Content-Type", "application/json"), body)
        except Exception as exc:
            err = json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}).encode()
            self._send(502, "application/json", err)


def main() -> None:
    print("CDSW_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_APP_PORT"))
    print("CDSW_READONLY_PORT = ", os.getenv("CDSW_READONLY_PORT"))
    print("CB_APP_PORT =", os.getenv("CB_APP_PORT"))
    print("UI_PORT =", UI_PORT)
    print("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT =", os.getenv("CDSW_PUBLIC_PORT"))
    
    server = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", UI_PORT), Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{UI_PORT}"
    print(f"  Compliance Bot UI  →  {url}")
    print(f"  Backend proxy      →  {BACKEND_URL}")
    print("  Press Ctrl+C to stop.\n")

    # Open browser after a short delay so the server is ready
    threading.Timer(0.8, lambda: webbrowser.open(url)).start()

    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nStopped.")
        sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


####################################################################################################
FILE: data\seed_synthetic_data.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Synthetic data generator for COMP_TBL.

Creates the database file, creates the table if absent, and inserts
realistic term-deposit compliance records into
``data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db``.  Run from the project root:

    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py              # create + insert 2000 rows
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --reset      # drop all rows then insert
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 500  # custom row count
    python data/seed_synthetic_data.py --count 0    # create DB/table only, no rows

The script uses only the Python standard library — no external packages needed.

Modular structure
-----------------
  ensure_database(db_path)          → creates the .db file and parent directories
  ensure_table(conn)                → creates COMP_TBL if it does not exist
  reset_table(conn)                 → deletes all existing rows
  generate_rows(count, start_i)     → returns a list of synthetic row tuples
  insert_rows(conn, rows)           → bulk-inserts rows
  print_summary(conn, db_path, n)   → prints statistics after insert
  seed(db_path, count, reset)       → orchestrates all of the above
"""

import argparse
import random
import sqlite3
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

# DDL and INSERT SQL kept here so schema is defined exactly once.

_CREATE_TABLE_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS COMP_TBL (
    BRCD               TEXT,
    CUST_NBR           TEXT,
    CUST_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR           TEXT,
    ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT    TEXT,
    ACCT_BRNCH         TEXT,
    BRTH_DT            TEXT,
    AGE_YRS            INTEGER,
    SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG   INTEGER,
    KYC_FLG            TEXT,
    END_OF_DAY_BAL     REAL,
    ACCT_OPN_DT        TEXT,
    MTRTY_DT           TEXT,
    tenor_yr           INTEGER,
    tenor_day          INTEGER,
    MAT_DT             TEXT,
    NEW_EFFCTV_RT      REAL,
    PRDCT_ID           TEXT,
    PF_FAC_REPT        TEXT,
    PRODUCT_NAME       TEXT,
    BRANCHNAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLENAME         TEXT,
    CIRCLECD           TEXT,
    EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT REAL,
    ISMOD              TEXT
)
"""

_INSERT_SQL = """
INSERT INTO COMP_TBL (
    BRCD, CUST_NBR, CUST_NBR_CHKDGT, ACCT_NBR, ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT, ACCT_BRNCH,
    BRTH_DT, AGE_YRS, SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG, KYC_FLG, END_OF_DAY_BAL, ACCT_OPN_DT,
    MTRTY_DT, tenor_yr, tenor_day, MAT_DT, NEW_EFFCTV_RT, PRDCT_ID, PF_FAC_REPT,
    PRODUCT_NAME, BRANCHNAME, CIRCLENAME, CIRCLECD, EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT, ISMOD
) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
"""

_REQUIRED_COLUMNS = {
    "BRCD", "CUST_NBR", "CUST_NBR_CHKDGT", "ACCT_NBR", "ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT",
    "ACCT_BRNCH", "BRTH_DT", "AGE_YRS", "SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG", "KYC_FLG",
    "END_OF_DAY_BAL", "ACCT_OPN_DT", "MTRTY_DT", "tenor_yr", "tenor_day",
    "MAT_DT", "NEW_EFFCTV_RT", "PRDCT_ID", "PF_FAC_REPT", "PRODUCT_NAME",
    "BRANCHNAME", "CIRCLENAME", "CIRCLECD", "EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT", "ISMOD",
}

# Step 1: create the .db file and its parent directory if they do not exist.

def ensure_database(db_path: Path) -> None:
    """
    Create the parent directory and SQLite database file if they do not exist.

    This is a no-op when both already exist — SQLite creates the file on
    first ``connect()`` so the function just guarantees the directory is there.

    Args:
        db_path: Absolute or relative path to the ``.db`` file.
    """
    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # SQLite creates the file on connect; touching it here just makes the
    # "created" message accurate.
    existed = db_path.exists()
    # open and close immediately to create the file
    sqlite3.connect(str(db_path)).close()
    if not existed:
        print(f"[create] Database file created: {db_path}")
    else:
        print(f"[ok]     Database file exists:  {db_path}")

# Step 2: create COMP_TBL with the correct 25-column schema if it is missing or stale.

def ensure_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Create ``COMP_TBL`` with the full 25-column schema if it does not exist.

    If the table already exists but is missing required columns (stale schema),
    it is dropped and recreated so the seeder can always run safely.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    table_exists = conn.execute(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='COMP_TBL'"
    ).fetchone()

    if table_exists:
        existing_cols = {
            row[1] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(COMP_TBL)").fetchall()
        }
        if not _REQUIRED_COLUMNS.issubset(existing_cols):
            conn.execute("DROP TABLE COMP_TBL")
            conn.commit()
            print("[migrate] COMP_TBL schema was stale — dropped and will be recreated.")
            table_exists = None  # fall through to CREATE

    if not table_exists:
        conn.execute(_CREATE_TABLE_SQL)
        conn.commit()
        print("[create] COMP_TBL created.")
    else:
        print("[ok]     COMP_TBL already exists with correct schema.")

# Step 3 (optional): wipe all existing rows when --reset is passed.

def reset_table(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """
    Delete every row from ``COMP_TBL``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection to the target database.
    """
    deleted = conn.execute("DELETE FROM COMP_TBL").rowcount
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[reset]  Deleted {deleted:,} existing rows from COMP_TBL.")

# Step 4: pure row-factory helpers — no database access, only random value generation.

def _rand_date(start: date, end: date) -> date:
    """Return a random date between *start* and *end* (inclusive)."""
    return start + timedelta(days=random.randint(0, (end - start).days))

def _fmt(d: date) -> str:
    return d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

def _rand_rate(base: float) -> float:
    """Return a rate within ±0.75 pp of *base*, rounded to 2 dp."""
    return round(base + random.uniform(-0.75, 0.75), 2)

def _make_row(i: int) -> tuple:
    """Generate one synthetic COMP_TBL row."""
    circle_name, circle_cd = random.choice(_CIRCLES)
    branch_name            = random.choice(_BRANCHES[circle_cd])
    prdct_id, product_name = random.choice(_PRODUCTS)

    age     = random.randint(18, 85)
    sr_flag = 1 if age >= 60 else 0
    dob     = _fmt(_rand_date(date(2025 - age - 1, 1, 1), date(2025 - age, 12, 31)))

    kyc     = "Y" if random.random() < (0.80 if sr_flag else 0.90) else "N"
    balance = round(random.lognormvariate(11.5, 1.2), 2)
    balance = max(10_000.0, min(5_000_000.0, balance))

    open_dt   = _rand_date(date(2005, 1, 1), date(2024, 6, 30))
    tenor_yr  = random.choice([1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 10, 15])
    tenor_day = random.randint(0, 364)
    mat_dt_d  = open_dt + timedelta(days=tenor_yr * 365 + tenor_day)
    mat_dt    = _fmt(mat_dt_d)

    is_mod  = "YES" if random.random() < 0.15 else "NO"
    mtrty_dt = _fmt(mat_dt_d + timedelta(days=random.randint(-30, 30))) if is_mod == "YES" else mat_dt

    base_rate = 7.0 if sr_flag else 6.5
    new_rate  = _rand_rate(base_rate)
    exp_rate  = _rand_rate(base_rate + 0.25)
    pf_fac    = "Y" if random.random() < 0.30 else "N"
    brcd      = f"{random.randint(1, 999):03d}"
    acct_brnch = f"M{random.randint(1, 99):03d}"

    return (
        brcd,                        # BRCD
        f"C{i:06d}",                 # CUST_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # CUST_NBR_CHKDGT
        f"A{i:06d}",                 # ACCT_NBR
        str(random.randint(1, 9)),   # ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT
        acct_brnch,                  # ACCT_BRNCH
        dob,                         # BRTH_DT
        age,                         # AGE_YRS
        sr_flag,                     # SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG
        kyc,                         # KYC_FLG
        balance,                     # END_OF_DAY_BAL
        _fmt(open_dt),               # ACCT_OPN_DT
        mtrty_dt,                    # MTRTY_DT
        tenor_yr,                    # tenor_yr
        tenor_day,                   # tenor_day
        mat_dt,                      # MAT_DT
        new_rate,                    # NEW_EFFCTV_RT
        prdct_id,                    # PRDCT_ID
        pf_fac,                      # PF_FAC_REPT
        product_name,                # PRODUCT_NAME
        branch_name,                 # BRANCHNAME
        circle_name,                 # CIRCLENAME
        circle_cd,                   # CIRCLECD
        exp_rate,                    # EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT
        is_mod,                      # ISMOD
    )

def generate_rows(count: int, start_i: int) -> list[tuple]:
    """
    Generate *count* synthetic row tuples starting from index *start_i*.

    ``start_i`` is used for ``CUST_NBR`` / ``ACCT_NBR`` sequential IDs so
    appending rows never collides with existing ones.

    Args:
        count:   Number of rows to generate.
        start_i: Index of the first new row (1-based).

    Returns:
        List of row tuples ready for ``executemany``.
    """
    return [_make_row(start_i + i) for i in range(count)]

# Step 5: bulk-insert the generated rows in a single executemany call.

def insert_rows(conn: sqlite3.Connection, rows: list[tuple]) -> None:
    """
    Bulk-insert *rows* into ``COMP_TBL`` using a single ``executemany``.

    Args:
        conn: Open SQLite connection (must already have COMP_TBL).
        rows: Row tuples as returned by :func:`generate_rows`.
    """
    conn.executemany(_INSERT_SQL, rows)
    conn.commit()
    print(f"[insert] {len(rows):,} rows inserted into COMP_TBL.")

# Step 6: query the DB and print row counts and top circles after the insert.

def print_summary(conn: sqlite3.Connection, db_path: Path, inserted: int) -> None:
    """
    Print a statistics summary after the insert.

    Args:
        conn:     Open SQLite connection.
        db_path:  Path to the database file (for display only).
        inserted: Number of rows that were just inserted.
    """
    total   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0]
    sr      = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1").fetchone()[0]
    kyc_n   = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'").fetchone()[0]
    circles = conn.execute(
        "SELECT CIRCLENAME, COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL GROUP BY CIRCLENAME ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 5"
    ).fetchall()

    print(f"\n{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Database : {db_path}")
    print(f"  Inserted : {inserted:,} rows")
    print(f"  Total    : {total:,} rows in COMP_TBL")
    print(f"{'='*55}")
    print(f"  Senior citizens  (SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG=1) : {sr:,}")
    print(f"  KYC non-compliant (KYC_FLG='N')        : {kyc_n:,}")
    print(f"\n  Top 5 circles by row count:")
    for name, cnt in circles:
        print(f"    {name:<22} {cnt:>5}")
    print(f"{'='*55}\n")

# Orchestrator that runs all six steps in sequence.

def seed(db_path: Path, count: int, reset: bool) -> None:
    """
    Full end-to-end pipeline:

    1. :func:`ensure_database` — create ``.db`` file and parent dirs if absent.
    2. :func:`ensure_table`    — create ``COMP_TBL`` if absent or stale.
    3. :func:`reset_table`     — (only when ``reset=True``) delete all rows.
    4. :func:`generate_rows`   — build synthetic row tuples in memory.
    5. :func:`insert_rows`     — bulk-insert into the database.
    6. :func:`print_summary`   — display post-insert statistics.

    Args:
        db_path: Path to the SQLite ``.db`` file.
        count:   Number of rows to insert (0 = create DB/table only).
        reset:   When ``True``, delete all existing rows before inserting.
    """
    # Step 1 — database file
    ensure_database(db_path)

    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))

    try:
        # Step 2 — table schema
        ensure_table(conn)

        # Step 3 — optional reset
        if reset:
            reset_table(conn)

        if count == 0:
            print("[info]   --count 0: skipping row insert.")
            print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=0)
            return

        # Step 4 — generate rows (start_i avoids CUST_NBR / ACCT_NBR collisions)
        start_i = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()[0] + 1
        rows = generate_rows(count, start_i)

        # Step 5 — insert
        insert_rows(conn, rows)

        # Step 6 — summary
        print_summary(conn, db_path, inserted=count)

    finally:
        conn.close()

# CLI argument parser and script entry-point.

def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Create the SQLite database + COMP_TBL schema and insert synthetic data.\n"
            "Safe to re-run: creates only what is missing."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--count", type=int, default=2000,
        help="Number of rows to insert (default: 2000). Use 0 to create DB/table only.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--reset", action="store_true",
        help="Delete all existing rows before inserting.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--db", type=Path,
        default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "comp_db" / "COMP_REPORTS.db",
        help="Path to the SQLite database file.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--seed", type=int, default=42,
        help="Random seed for reproducibility (default: 42).",
    )
    return p.parse_args()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = _parse_args()
    random.seed(args.seed)
    seed(db_path=args.db, count=args.count, reset=args.reset)

### Cleaned SQL Schema

```sql
CREATE TABLE "COMP_TBL" (
    "CUST_NBR" FLOAT,
    "CUST_NBR_CHKDGT" FLOAT,
    "ACCT_NBR" FLOAT,
    "ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT" FLOAT,
    "ACCT_BRNCH" FLOAT,
    "BRTH_DT" DATETIME,
    "AGE_YRS" FLOAT,
    "SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG" FLOAT,
    "KYC_FLG" TEXT,
    "END_OF_DAY_BAL" FLOAT,
    "ACCT_OPN_DT" DATETIME,
    "MTRTY_DT" DATETIME,
    "tenor_yr" BIGINT,
    "tenor_day" BIGINT,
    "MAT_DT" DATETIME,
    "NEW_EFFCTV_RT" FLOAT,
    "PRDCT_ID" FLOAT,
    "PF_FAC_REPT" TEXT,
    "PRODUCT_NAME" TEXT,
    "BRANCHNAME" TEXT,
    "CIRCLENAME" TEXT,
    "CIRCLECD" FLOAT,
    "EXPECTED_EFFCTV_RT" FLOAT,
    "ISMOD" TEXT
);
```

### Data Observations
While the data section is scrambled because it's binary, I can extract some of the actual values present in your file for your reference:

*   **Product Names:** `PUB IND UNI 181D-10YRS`, `TDTDR PUB IND UNI-91-179D-INR`
*   **Branch Names:** `VIKRAM SARABHAI BHAWAN`, `MANDVI MUMBAI`, `KHOPAT BRANCH THANE`, `WADALA (MUMBAI)`, `DHARAVI (MUMBAI)`, `ACB BARAGAON`, `CHAKALA ROAD BRANCH`, `NALLASOPARA - WEST`, `PANVEL`.
*   **Circle Name:** `MUMBAI METRO`, `LUCKNOW`.
*   **Dates found:** `1961-09-04`, `2011-08-30`, `2026-08-30`, `2027-08-31`, etc.

**Note:** If you need to extract the actual data into a spreadsheet or a clean table, you cannot do it by copying and pasting the text. You must open the original `.db` or `.sqlite` file using a tool like **DB Browser for SQLite** or by using Python's `sqlite3` library.

"""
genai — Enterprise GenAI package for the Compliance Chatbot.

Organises all AI/ML logic into three sub-packages:
  - genai.utils       : shared helpers (logging, constants, config/prompt loaders)
  - genai.core        : compliance orchestration, LLM provider, schemas, exceptions
  - genai.nl2sql_agent: six-node LangGraph NL2SQL pipeline
"""

__version__ = "2.0.0"
__all__ = ["__version__"]



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\config\application.toml
####################################################################################################

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

version     = "2.0.0"

[server]
host      = "0.0.0.0"
port      = 8000

####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\config\databases.toml
####################################################################################################

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# databases.toml — database and table registry
#
# HOW TO ADD A NEW DATABASE / TABLE
# ──────────────────────────────────
# 1. Add a new [databases.<DB_STEM>] block with db_file and description.
# 2. Add one [databases.<DB_STEM>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>] block per table with:
#      description   — plain-English summary used in the LLM prompt
#      filter_query  — optional parameterised filter template
# 3. Add [databases.<DB_STEM>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.columns] with
#      COLUMN_NAME = "Human-readable description"
#    for every column the LLM should know about.
# 4. Add [databases.<DB_STEM>.tables.<TABLE_NAME>.few_shots] with
#      examples = """..."""  (Question/Response pairs, one per block)
#
# NO Python code changes are needed — the pipeline picks up new entries
# automatically on next startup.
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Database: COMP_REPORTS  (data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db)
# ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

[databases.COMP_REPORTS]
db_file     = "data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db"
description = "Banking compliance reports database for term-deposit products."

# ── Table: COMP_TBL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL]
description   = "Compliance table for term-deposit products targeting senior-citizen customers."
filter_query  = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE ACCT_BRNCH IN ({branch_code})"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.columns]
BRCD               = "BRANCH CODE OR BRANCH NUMBER"
CUST_NBR           = "CUSTOMER NUMBER — UNIQUE IDENTIFIER ASSIGNED TO EACH CUSTOMER IN THE BANKING SYSTEM"
CUST_NBR_CHKDGT    = "CUSTOMER NUMBER CHECK DIGIT"
ACCT_NBR           = "UNIQUE IDENTIFIER FOR A SPECIFIC BANK ACCOUNT"
ACCT_NBR_CHKDGT    = "ACCOUNT NUMBER CHECK DIGIT"
ACCT_BRNCH         = "ACCOUNT BRANCH — IDENTIFIES THE BRANCH WHERE THE ACCOUNT IS MAINTAINED OR HELD"
BRTH_DT            = "BIRTH DATE — CUSTOMER DATE OF BIRTH, USED FOR AGE CALCULATION AND KYC PURPOSES"

ACCT_OPN_DT        = "ACCOUNT OPEN DATE — THE DATE WHEN THE BANK ACCOUNT WAS INITIALLY OPENED"

NEW_EFFCTV_RT      = "NEW EFFECTIVE RATE — THE UPDATED INTEREST RATE APPLIED TO THE ACCOUNT OR PRODUCT"
PRDCT_ID           = "PRODUCT ID — IDENTIFIER FOR THE FINANCIAL PRODUCT (E.G. SAVINGS, FIXED DEPOSIT) LINKED TO THE ACCOUNT"
PF_FAC_REPT        = "PENSION FUND FACILITY REPORTING — INDICATOR OR DATA RELATED TO PENSION FUND REPORTING REQUIREMENTS"
PRODUCT_NAME       = "HUMAN-READABLE NAME OF THE FINANCIAL PRODUCT ASSOCIATED WITH THE ACCOUNT"
BRANCHNAME         = "FULL NAME OF THE BRANCH WHERE THE ACCOUNT IS MAINTAINED"

[databases.COMP_REPORTS.tables.COMP_TBL.few_shots]
examples = """

Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1 AND AGE_YRS > 60;
<end_sql_response>

Question: Show the customers whose age is less than 60 and product name contains 'SR CT'.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE UPPER(PRODUCT_NAME) LIKE '%SR CT%' AND AGE_YRS < 60;
<end_sql_response>

<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE MTRTY_DT BETWEEN '2025-12-01' AND '2025-12-31';
<end_sql_response>

Question: List all accounts where KYC flag is N.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N';
<end_sql_response>

Question: Show the top 10 accounts with the highest end-of-day balance.
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL ORDER BY END_OF_DAY_BAL DESC LIMIT 10;
<end_sql_response>

Question: How many senior citizen accounts are there in the Mumbai circle?
Response:
<begin_sql_response>
SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1 AND UPPER(CIRCLENAME) LIKE '%MUMBAI%';
<end_sql_response>
"""

####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\config\query.toml
####################################################################################################

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# query.toml — row-limit and query execution settings
#
# enforce_default_limit
#   true  → when the LLM-generated SQL has no LIMIT clause, automatically
#            append "LIMIT <default_limit>" before executing.
#   false → execute the SQL as-is, no automatic LIMIT is added.
#
# default_limit
#   Number of rows to show when no LIMIT is present in the SQL and
#   enforce_default_limit = true.
#
# max_display_rows
#   Hard ceiling on rows returned to the API caller regardless of any LIMIT
#   in the SQL.  Acts as a safety net.  Set to 0 to disable.
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

[query]
enforce_default_limit = false
default_limit         = 10
max_display_rows      = 50
