####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\utils\config.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/utils/config.py — application settings and shared constants.

Merges:
  - config_loader.py  (Pydantic BaseSettings — loads from .env)
  - constants.py      (application-wide literal constants)

All secrets (API keys) must be provided via .env or environment variables.
TOML config files under genai/config/ provide default values;
environment variables always take precedence.
"""

from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict

# ── Shared literal constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

# SQL delimiter tags the LLM must wrap its generated SQL between.
SQL_BEGIN_TAG: str = "<begin_sql_response>"
SQL_END_TAG: str   = "<end_sql_response>"

# Primary compliance table name used across the pipeline.
COMPLIANCE_TABLE_NAME: str      = "COMP_TBL"
BRANCH_CODE_PLACEHOLDER: str    = "{branch_code}"

# API response dictionary key names.
RESPONSE_QUESTION_ID: str = "question_id"
RESPONSE_SQL_QUERY: str   = "sql_query"
RESPONSE_DF_OUTPUT: str   = "df_output"

# Root logger name shared by all modules.
LOGGER_ROOT_NAME: str = "compliance_bot"

# Default LLM inference parameters.
DEFAULT_MAX_NEW_TOKENS: int       = 512
DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE: float        = 0.1
DEFAULT_STOP_SEQUENCES: list[str] = [SQL_END_TAG]


# ── Pydantic settings models ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DatabaseSettings(BaseSettings):
    """Database connection configuration."""

    db_type:   str = Field(default="SQLite",        description="Database engine type.")
    db_folder: str = Field(default="data/comp_db",  description="Relative path to the DB folder.")
    db_name:   str = Field(default="COMP_REPORTS.db", description="SQLite database filename.")

    @property
    def db_path(self) -> Path:
        """Resolve absolute path to the SQLite database file."""
        return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / self.db_folder / self.db_name


class ModelSettings(BaseSettings):
    """LLM configuration for the Compliance Chatbot."""

    provider: str = Field(default="dlh", description="LLM provider: 'dlh' or 'ica'.")
    model_name: str = Field(
        default="google/gemma-4-31b-it",
        description="Remote model name for OpenAI-compatible providers.",
    )
    model_max_new_tokens: int   = Field(default=512,  description="Maximum tokens to generate.")
    model_temperature:    float = Field(default=0.1,  description="Sampling temperature.")
    dlh_base_url: str = Field(
        default="http://10.190.236.15:9000/api/v1",
        description="DLH inference server base URL.",
    )
    dlh_api_key:    str  = Field(default="EMPTY", description="Bearer token for the DLH endpoint.")
    dlh_verify_ssl: bool = Field(default=False,   description="Verify TLS certificates for DLH.")
    ica_base_url: str = Field(
        default="https://api.nextgen-beta.ica.ibm.com",
        description="ICA API host URL.",
    )
    ica_api_key:          str  = Field(default="",           description="Bearer key for ICA APIs.")
    ica_namespace:        str  = Field(default="chat-models", description="ICA namespace.")
    ica_send_temperature: bool = Field(
        default=False,
        description="Whether to forward temperature/max_tokens to ICA.",
    )


class AppSettings(BaseSettings):
    """Top-level application configuration."""

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        env_nested_delimiter="__",
        extra="ignore",
        populate_by_name=True,
    )

    application_name: str  = Field(default="COMPLIANCE-BOT (UI + LLM Hybrid)")
    page_config:      str  = Field(default="SBI COMPLIANCE-BOT")
    log_level:        str  = Field(default="INFO")
    log_dir:          str  = Field(default="data/logs")
    host:             str  = Field(default="0.0.0.0")
    port:             int  = Field(default=8000)
    debug:            bool = Field(default=False)

    database: DatabaseSettings = Field(default_factory=DatabaseSettings)
    model:    ModelSettings    = Field(default_factory=ModelSettings)


# Module-level singleton — instantiated once on first import.
_settings: AppSettings = AppSettings()


def get_settings() -> AppSettings:
    """Return the cached application settings singleton."""
    return _settings



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\utils\helpers.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/utils/helpers.py — stateless utility helpers.

Merges:
  - common.py    (safe_json_loads, truncate, normalise_whitespace, flatten_list)
  - file_utils.py (ensure_directory, read/write text/bytes, safe_delete, list_files …)
  - json_utils.py (safe_loads, safe_dumps, extract_json_from_text, …)

Nothing in this file has side-effects or external dependencies beyond the
Python standard library.
"""

import csv
import io
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


# ── General helpers (previously common.py) ────────────────────────────────────

def safe_json_loads(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Try to parse text as JSON; return None if parsing fails."""
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return None


def truncate(text: str, max_len: int = 300, suffix: str = "…") -> str:
    """Shorten text to max_len characters and append suffix if it was cut."""
    if len(text) <= max_len:
        return text
    return text[: max_len - len(suffix)] + suffix


def normalise_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse any run of whitespace (spaces, tabs, newlines) into a single space."""
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()


def flatten_list(nested: List[Any]) -> List[Any]:
    """
    Flatten exactly one level of nesting in a list.

    Example: [[1, 2], [3], 4] → [1, 2, 3, 4]
    """
    result: List[Any] = []
    for item in nested:
        if isinstance(item, list):
            result.extend(item)
        else:
            result.append(item)
    return result


# ── File system helpers (previously file_utils.py) ────────────────────────────

def ensure_directory(path: "str | Path") -> Path:
    """Create the directory (and any missing parents) if it doesn't exist yet."""
    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    resolved.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return resolved


def read_text(path: "str | Path", encoding: str = "utf-8") -> str:
    """Read a text file and return its contents as a string."""
    return Path(path).read_text(encoding=encoding)


def write_text(path: "str | Path", content: str, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> Path:
    """Write content to a file, creating parent directories if needed."""
    dest = Path(path).resolve()
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_text(content, encoding=encoding)
    return dest


def read_bytes(path: "str | Path") -> bytes:
    """Read a file and return its raw bytes."""
    return Path(path).read_bytes()


def write_bytes(path: "str | Path", data: bytes) -> Path:
    """Write binary data to a file, creating parent directories if needed."""
    dest = Path(path).resolve()
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_bytes(data)
    return dest


def safe_delete(path: "str | Path") -> bool:
    """
    Delete a file or entire directory tree without raising if it doesn't exist.

    Returns True if something was deleted, False if the path was already gone.
    """
    target = Path(path)
    if not target.exists():
        return False
    if target.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(target)
    else:
        target.unlink()
    return True


def list_files(
    directory: "str | Path",
    pattern: str = "*",
    recursive: bool = False,
) -> List[Path]:
    """
    Return a sorted list of files matching pattern inside directory.

    Set recursive=True to search all subdirectories as well.
    """
    root = Path(directory)
    globber = root.rglob if recursive else root.glob
    return sorted(p for p in globber(pattern) if p.is_file())


def file_size(path: "str | Path") -> int:
    """Return the byte size of a file, or 0 if the file does not exist."""
    target = Path(path)
    return target.stat().st_size if target.exists() else 0


def resolve_project_path(*parts: str) -> Path:
    """
    Build an absolute path relative to the project root.

    The project root is two levels above genai/utils/ (workspace root).
    Example: resolve_project_path("data", "comp_db", "COMP_REPORTS.db")
    """
    project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    return project_root.joinpath(*parts)


# ── JSON helpers (previously json_utils.py) ───────────────────────────────────

# Matches the first JSON object {...} or array [...] found inside free text.
_JSON_OBJECT_PATTERN: re.Pattern[str] = re.compile(r"(\{.*?\}|\[.*?\])", re.DOTALL)


def safe_loads(text: str) -> Optional[Any]:
    """Parse text as JSON; return None instead of raising on bad input."""
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        return None


def safe_dumps(obj: Any, indent: int = 2, default: Any = str) -> str:
    """Serialise obj to JSON, converting un-serialisable values with str()."""
    return json.dumps(obj, indent=indent, default=default, ensure_ascii=False)


def extract_json_from_text(text: str) -> Optional[Any]:
    """
    Scan free-form text for the first valid JSON object or array and return it.

    Useful when the LLM wraps its JSON answer in explanatory prose.
    Returns None if no valid JSON block is found.
    """
    for match in _JSON_OBJECT_PATTERN.finditer(text):
        parsed = safe_loads(match.group(0))
        if parsed is not None:
            return parsed
    return None


def extract_json_array(text: str) -> List[Any]:
    """Return the first JSON array found in text, or [] if none exists."""
    result = extract_json_from_text(text)
    if isinstance(result, list):
        return result
    return []


def extract_json_object(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the first JSON object found in text, or {} if none exists."""
    result = extract_json_from_text(text)
    if isinstance(result, dict):
        return result
    return {}


def pretty_print(obj: Any) -> str:
    """Return a nicely indented JSON string for obj."""
    return safe_dumps(obj, indent=2)


def is_valid_json(text: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if text is syntactically valid JSON, False otherwise."""
    return safe_loads(text) is not None



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\utils\logger.py
####################################################################################################

"""
Centralised logging factory for the Compliance Chatbot.

All modules must obtain their logger exclusively through :func:`get_logger`
to guarantee consistent formatting and handler configuration.

Configuration is loaded from ``genai/config/logging.toml`` (TOML-based,
Python 3.11+ tomllib).  Falls back to a minimal ``basicConfig`` when the
TOML file is absent or unreadable.
"""

import logging
import logging.config
import logging.handlers
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

_LOGGING_CONFIGURED: bool = False

# TOML-based logging config (preferred).
_TOML_CONFIG_PATH: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "genai" / "config" / "logging.toml"


def _load_toml_logging_config(path: Path) -> Optional[dict]:
    """Load logging configuration from a TOML file. Returns None on failure."""
    try:
        try:
            import tomllib  # Python 3.11+
        except ImportError:
            try:
                import tomli as tomllib  # type: ignore[no-redef]
            except ImportError:
                return None

        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            data = tomllib.load(fh)
        return data.get("logging")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return None


def _configure_logging(log_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """
    Bootstrap logging from ``genai/config/logging.toml``.

    Falls back to a minimal ``basicConfig`` if the TOML file is absent or
    the tomllib/tomli package is not available.

    Args:
        log_dir: Optional override for the log output directory.
    """
    global _LOGGING_CONFIGURED  # pylint: disable=global-statement

    if _LOGGING_CONFIGURED:
        return

    # Ensure the log directory exists.
    resolved_log_dir = Path(log_dir) if log_dir else Path("data/logs")
    resolved_log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    log_config = _load_toml_logging_config(_TOML_CONFIG_PATH)

    if log_config:
        # Patch the rotating_file handler filename if a custom log_dir was given.
        handlers = log_config.get("handlers", {})
        rotating = handlers.get("rotating_file")
        if rotating:
            rotating["filename"] = str(resolved_log_dir / "compliance_bot.log")

        try:
            logging.config.dictConfig(log_config)
            _LOGGING_CONFIGURED = True
            return
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            pass  # Fall through to basicConfig

    # Fallback: simple basicConfig.
    logging.basicConfig(
        level=logging.INFO,
        format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
    )
    logging.getLogger(__name__).warning(
        "Logging TOML config not found or unreadable at %s — using basicConfig fallback.",
        _TOML_CONFIG_PATH,
    )

    _LOGGING_CONFIGURED = True


def get_logger(name: str, log_dir: Optional[str] = None) -> logging.Logger:
    """
    Return a named logger, ensuring logging is configured beforehand.

    Args:
        name:    Dot-separated logger name (e.g. ``"compliance_bot.core"``).
        log_dir: Optional log output directory; used only on first call.

    Returns:
        Configured :class:`logging.Logger` instance.
    """
    _configure_logging(log_dir=log_dir)
    return logging.getLogger(name)



####################################################################################################
FILE: genai\utils\registry.py
####################################################################################################

"""
genai/utils/registry.py — data-access registries: databases, prompts, SQLite helpers.

Merges:
  - db_registry.py   — databases.toml metadata registry (tables, columns, few-shots)
  - db_utils.py      — read-only SQLite connection + query helpers
  - prompt_loader.py — TOML prompt file loader with in-memory cache

All three share the same concern: reading persistent data stores (TOML files
and SQLite databases) and caching the results in memory.
"""

import sqlite3
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Generator, List, Optional, Tuple

from genai.utils.logger import get_logger

LOGGER = get_logger("compliance_bot.utils.registry")

# ── TOML loading helper (shared by db_registry and prompt_loader) ─────────────

def _load_toml_file(path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Open and parse a TOML file; return {} on any failure."""
    try:
        try:
            import tomllib          # built-in Python 3.11+
        except ImportError:
            import tomli as tomllib  # type: ignore[no-redef]
        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            return tomllib.load(fh)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        LOGGER.warning("registry._load_toml_file — failed to load %s: %s", path, exc)
        return {}


# ── Database and table metadata registry (previously db_registry.py) ─────────

# Location of the TOML file that describes all registered databases.
_DATABASES_TOML = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "config" / "databases.toml"

# In-memory cache — built lazily on first access, cleared by reload().
_db_registry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None


def _build_db_registry() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Parse databases.toml and return a flat {table_name: metadata} dict."""
    if not _DATABASES_TOML.exists():
        LOGGER.warning("registry: databases.toml not found at %s", _DATABASES_TOML)
        return {}

    raw = _load_toml_file(_DATABASES_TOML)
    registry: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    for db_stem, db_data in raw.get("databases", {}).items():
        db_file = db_data.get("db_file", "")
        for table_name, table_data in db_data.get("tables", {}).items():
            few_shots_raw = table_data.get("few_shots", {})
            few_shots_str = (
                few_shots_raw.get("examples", "").strip()
                if isinstance(few_shots_raw, dict)
                else ""
            )
            registry[table_name] = {
                "db_stem":      db_stem,
                "db_file":      db_file,
                "description":  table_data.get("description", ""),
                "filter_query": table_data.get("filter_query", ""),
                "columns":      table_data.get("columns", {}),
                "few_shots":    few_shots_str,
            }
            LOGGER.debug("registry: registered table %s (db=%s)", table_name, db_stem)

    LOGGER.info("registry: loaded %d table(s) from databases.toml", len(registry))
    return registry


def _get_db_registry() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the cached registry, building it on first call."""
    global _db_registry  # noqa: PLW0603
    if _db_registry is None:
        _db_registry = _build_db_registry()
    return _db_registry


def reload() -> None:
    """Clear the database registry cache so the next access re-reads databases.toml."""
    global _db_registry  # noqa: PLW0603
    _db_registry = None
    LOGGER.info("registry: db cache cleared — will reload on next access.")


def get_table_meta(table_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the full metadata dict for a registered table, or {} if unknown."""
    return _get_db_registry().get(table_name, {})


def get_few_shots(table_name: str) -> str:
    """Return the few-shot example block for a table, or '' if not registered."""
    return _get_db_registry().get(table_name, {}).get("few_shots", "")


def get_all_tables() -> List[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of every registered table name."""
    return sorted(_get_db_registry().keys())


def list_databases() -> List[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of every registered database stem name."""
    reg = _get_db_registry()
    return sorted({meta["db_stem"] for meta in reg.values()})


# ── Read-only SQLite helpers (previously db_utils.py) ─────────────────────────

@contextmanager
def open_readonly(db_path: "str | Path") -> Generator[sqlite3.Connection, None, None]:
    """
    Context manager: open a SQLite file in deferred read-only mode.

    Always rolls back and closes the connection on exit — even on error.
    Usage:
        with open_readonly("data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db") as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL").fetchone()
    """
    path = Path(db_path)
    conn: Optional[sqlite3.Connection] = None
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("BEGIN DEFERRED")
        yield conn
    finally:
        if conn is not None:
            try:
                conn.rollback()
            except sqlite3.Error:
                pass
            conn.close()


def fetch_all(
    db_path: "str | Path",
    sql: str,
    params: Tuple[Any, ...] = (),
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Run sql and return every result row as a list of {column: value} dicts."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        cursor = conn.execute(sql, params)
        col_names: List[str] = [d[0] for d in (cursor.description or [])]
        return [dict(zip(col_names, row)) for row in cursor.fetchall()]


def fetch_scalar(
    db_path: "str | Path",
    sql: str,
    params: Tuple[Any, ...] = (),
    default: Any = None,
) -> Any:
    """Run sql and return just the first column of the first row (e.g. COUNT(*))."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        row = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchone()
    return row[0] if row else default


def list_tables(db_path: "str | Path") -> List[str]:
    """Return a sorted list of all table names in the database."""
    sql = "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name"
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(sql).fetchall()
    return [row[0] for row in rows]


def table_exists(db_path: "str | Path", table_name: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if table_name exists in the database (case-insensitive check)."""
    sql = "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND UPPER(name)=UPPER(?)"
    return fetch_scalar(db_path, sql, (table_name,)) is not None


def get_column_names(db_path: "str | Path", table_name: str) -> List[str]:
    """Return the ordered column names for a table via PRAGMA table_info."""
    with open_readonly(db_path) as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info('{table_name}')").fetchall()
    return [row[1] for row in rows]


def row_count(db_path: "str | Path", table_name: str) -> int:
    """Return how many rows are in the table; returns 0 on any error."""
    try:
        return fetch_scalar(db_path, f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM '{table_name}'", default=0)
    except sqlite3.Error as err:
        LOGGER.warning("registry.row_count — error on %s.%s: %s", db_path, table_name, err)
        return 0


# ── TOML prompt loader (previously prompt_loader.py) ─────────────────────────

# All TOML prompt files live here.
_PROMPTS_DIR: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "prompts"

# Parse results are kept here so each file is read only once.
_prompt_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}


def load_prompt(filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Load and cache a TOML prompt file from genai/prompts/.

    filename — e.g. "sql_writer.toml"
    Returns the full parsed dict, or {} if tomllib is unavailable.
    Raises FileNotFoundError if the file does not exist.
    """
    stem = filename.removesuffix(".toml")

    if stem in _prompt_cache:
        return _prompt_cache[stem]

    path = _PROMPTS_DIR / (stem + ".toml")
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Prompt file not found: {path}")

    data = _load_toml_file(path)
    _prompt_cache[stem] = data
    LOGGER.debug("registry: loaded prompt file: %s", path)
    return data


def get_prompt(filename: str, *keys: str, default: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Retrieve a nested string value from a TOML prompt file.

    Example: get_prompt("intent_checker.toml", "messages", "clarification")

    Walks the key path and returns the string at that location,
    or default if any key is missing.
    """
    data = load_prompt(filename)
    node: Any = data
    for key in keys:
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            return default
        node = node.get(key)
        if node is None:
            return default
    return str(node) if node is not None else default

    "## Section 8 — Intent Agent\n",
    "Tests 51–66: clear intent questions pass; empty/vague/disallowed questions are blocked; normalisation adds `?`."
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "from genai.nl2sql_agent.intent import IntentAgent\n",
    "from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState\n",
    "\n",
    "_ia = IntentAgent()\n",
    "\n",
    "def _intent(question):\n",
    "    return _ia.run(AgentState(question=question))  # type: ignore\n",
    "\n",
    "def t51(): assert _intent(\"How many accounts have KYC flag N?\")[\"intent_ok\"] is True\n",
    "def t52(): assert _intent(\"How many accounts have KYC flag N?\").get(\"clarification_needed\") is None\n",
    "def t53(): assert _intent(\"Show all senior citizen accounts in Mumbai circle.\")[\"intent_ok\"] is True\n",
    "def t54(): assert _intent(\"List accounts with balance above 100000.\")[\"intent_ok\"] is True\n",
    "def t55(): assert _intent(\"What is the maturity date for senior citizen accounts in Thane branch?\")[\"intent_ok\"] is True\n",
    "def t56(): assert _intent(\"How many accounts are in the Thane branch?\")[\"intent_ok\"] is True\n",
    "\n",
    "def t57():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and r[\"clarification_needed\"]\n",
    "def t58(): assert _intent(\"   \")[\"intent_ok\"] is False\n",
    "\n",
    "def t59():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"Please delete all records from the table.\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and \"DELETE\" in (r.get(\"clarification_needed\") or \"\")\n",
    "def t60():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"drop all data\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and \"DROP\" in (r.get(\"clarification_needed\") or \"\")\n",
    "def t61():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"insert records\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and \"INSERT\" in (r.get(\"clarification_needed\") or \"\")\n",
    "def t62():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"update the balance\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and \"UPDATE\" in (r.get(\"clarification_needed\") or \"\")\n",
    "def t63():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"truncate the table\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"intent_ok\"] is False and \"TRUNCATE\" in (r.get(\"clarification_needed\") or \"\")\n",
    "\n",
    "def t64(): assert _intent(\"give me data\")[\"intent_ok\"] is False\n",
    "def t65(): assert _intent(\"something\")[\"intent_ok\"] is False\n",
    "\n",
    "def t66():\n",
    "    r = _intent(\"List KYC flag N accounts\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"interpreted_question\", \"\").endswith(\"?\")\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"Section 8 — Intent Agent (Tests 51–66)\")\n",
    "run_test(51, \"KYC question → intent_ok True\",                            t51)\n",
    "run_test(52, \"KYC question → clarification_needed is None\",              t52)\n",
    "run_test(53, \"Senior citizen question → intent_ok True\",                 t53)\n",
    "run_test(54, \"Balance question → intent_ok True\",                        t54)\n",
    "run_test(55, \"Maturity date question → intent_ok True\",                  t55)\n",
    "run_test(56, \"Count query → intent_ok True\",                             t56)\n",
    "run_test(57, \"Empty string → intent_ok False + clarification set\",       t57)\n",
    "run_test(58, \"Whitespace-only → intent_ok False\",                        t58)\n",
    "run_test(59, \"'delete' keyword → blocked with DELETE in message\",        t59)\n",
    "run_test(60, \"'drop' keyword → blocked with DROP in message\",            t60)\n",
    "run_test(61, \"'insert' keyword → blocked with INSERT in message\",        t61)\n",
    "run_test(62, \"'update' keyword → blocked with UPDATE in message\",        t62)\n",
    "run_test(63, \"'truncate' keyword → blocked with TRUNCATE in message\",    t63)\n",
    "run_test(64, \"'give me data' (vague) → intent_ok False\",                 t64)\n",
    "run_test(65, \"'something' (too short/ambiguous) → intent_ok False\",      t65)\n",
    "run_test(66, \"Normalisation adds trailing question mark\",                t66)"
   ]

    "## Section 10 — SQL Review Agent\n",
    "Tests 72–84: valid queries pass; DML keywords, structural problems, unknown tables, and max-retries are all rejected."
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_review import SQLReviewAgent\n",
    "\n",
    "_ra = SQLReviewAgent(max_retries=3)\n",
    "\n",
    "def _review(sql, retry=0):\n",
    "    return _ra.run(AgentState(sql_query=sql, db_path=str(TMP_DB), retry_count=retry))  # type: ignore\n",
    "\n",
    "def t72(): assert _review(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is True\n",
    "def t73(): assert _review(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL\").get(\"review_feedback\") is None\n",
    "def t74(): assert _review(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is True\n",
    "def t75(): assert _review(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is True\n",
    "\n",
    "def t76():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"DELETE FROM COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False and r[\"retry_count\"] == 1\n",
    "def t77():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"DROP TABLE COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "def t78():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1)\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "def t79():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"UPDATE COMP_TBL SET AGE_YRS=1\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "def t80():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"TRUNCATE COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "\n",
    "def t81(): assert _review(\"SELECT 1\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False          # missing FROM\n",
    "def t82(): assert _review(\"WITH cte AS (SELECT 1) SHOW cte\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "def t83(): assert _review(\"\")[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False\n",
    "\n",
    "def t84():\n",
    "    r = _review(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL\", retry=3)    # at max retries\n",
    "    assert r[\"sql_review_passed\"] is False and r.get(\"error\")\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"Section 10 — SQL Review Agent (Tests 72–84)\")\n",
    "run_test(72, \"Simple SELECT passes review\",                            t72)\n",
    "run_test(73, \"Passing SELECT has no feedback\",                         t73)\n",
    "run_test(74, \"SELECT with WHERE clause passes\",                        t74)\n",
    "run_test(75, \"Aggregate COUNT(*) query passes\",                        t75)\n",
    "run_test(76, \"DELETE keyword rejected; retry_count incremented\",       t76)\n",
    "run_test(77, \"DROP keyword rejected\",                                  t77)\n",
    "run_test(78, \"INSERT keyword rejected\",                                t78)\n",
    "run_test(79, \"UPDATE keyword rejected\",                                t79)\n",
    "run_test(80, \"TRUNCATE keyword rejected\",                              t80)\n",
    "run_test(81, \"Missing FROM clause rejected\",                           t81)\n",
    "run_test(82, \"No SELECT prefix rejected\",                              t82)\n",
    "run_test(83, \"Empty SQL rejected\",                                     t83)\n",
    "run_test(84, \"Max retries exceeded → error set\",                       t84)"
   ]
  },

    "## Section 11 — SQL Validator Agent\n",
    "Tests 85–91: sqlglot AST validation — valid SELECT passes, INSERT fails, retry_count incremented on failure."
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_validator import SQLValidatorAgent\n",
    "\n",
    "_va = SQLValidatorAgent(dialect=\"sqlite\")\n",
    "\n",
    "def _validate(sql, retry=0):\n",
    "    return _va.run(AgentState(sql_query=sql, retry_count=retry))  # type: ignore\n",
    "\n",
    "def t85():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is True and r.get(\"sqlglot_error\") is None\n",
    "def t86():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is True\n",
    "def t87():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL ORDER BY END_OF_DAY_BAL DESC LIMIT 10\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is True\n",
    "\n",
    "def t88():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"SELECT * FROM WHERE = BROKEN\")\n",
    "    if r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is False:\n",
    "        assert r.get(\"sqlglot_error\")\n",
    "def t89():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1, 2)\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is False\n",
    "\n",
    "def t90():\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1)\", retry=1)\n",
    "    if r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\") is False:\n",
    "        assert r.get(\"retry_count\", 0) > 1\n",
    "\n",
    "def t91():\n",
    "    # sqlglot_valid should always be bool\n",
    "    r = _validate(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert isinstance(r.get(\"sqlglot_valid\"), bool)\n",
    "\n",
    "print(\"Section 11 — SQL Validator Agent (Tests 85–91)\")\n",
    "run_test(85, \"SELECT with WHERE → sqlglot_valid True, no error\",      t85)\n",
    "run_test(86, \"COUNT(*) aggregate → sqlglot_valid True\",               t86)\n",
    "run_test(87, \"ORDER BY + LIMIT → sqlglot_valid True\",                 t87)\n",
    "run_test(88, \"Broken SQL → fail with sqlglot_error set\",              t88)\n",
    "run_test(89, \"INSERT statement → sqlglot_valid False\",                t89)\n",
    "run_test(90, \"INSERT with retry=1 → retry_count incremented\",         t90)\n",
    "run_test(91, \"sqlglot_valid is always a boolean\",                     t91)"
   ]
  },

    "## Section 12 — Executor Agent\n",
    "Tests 92–102: valid SELECTs return rows; error cases return error messages; LIMIT injection policy."
   ]
  },
  {
   "cell_type": "code",
   "execution_count": null,
   "metadata": {},
   "outputs": [],
   "source": [
    "from genai.nl2sql_agent.executor import ExecutorAgent, _ensure_limit, _ENFORCE_LIMIT\n",
    "\n",
    "_ea = ExecutorAgent()\n",
    "\n",
    "def _exec(sql, db=None):\n",
    "    return _ea.run(AgentState(sql_query=sql, db_path=str(db or TMP_DB)))  # type: ignore\n",
    "\n",
    "def t92():\n",
    "    r = _exec(\"SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"error\") is None\n",
    "    assert len(r[\"result_rows\"]) > 0\n",
    "\n",
    "def t93():\n",
    "    r = _exec(\"SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM COMP_TBL\")\n",
    "    assert r.get(\"error\") is None\n",
    "    assert len(r[\"result_rows\"]) == 1 and \"cnt\" in r[\"result_rows\"][0]\n",
    "\n",
    "def t94():\n",

"""
tests/conftest.py — shared fixtures for the Compliance Chatbot test suite.
"""

import sqlite3
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest


# ── In-memory SQLite helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

@pytest.fixture
def tmp_db(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """
    Create a temporary SQLite database at *tmp_path/COMP_REPORTS.db* with
    a minimal COMP_TBL table and five seed rows.

    Returns:
        Path to the temporary database file.
    """
    db_path = tmp_path / "COMP_REPORTS.db"
    with sqlite3.connect(str(db_path)) as conn:
        conn.execute(
            """
            CREATE TABLE COMP_TBL (
                BRCD              TEXT,
                CUST_NBR          TEXT,
                ACCT_NBR          TEXT,
                ACCT_BRNCH        TEXT,
                AGE_YRS           INTEGER,
                SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG  INTEGER,
                KYC_FLG           TEXT,
                END_OF_DAY_BAL    REAL,
                MTRTY_DT          TEXT,
                PRODUCT_NAME      TEXT,
                BRANCHNAME        TEXT,
                CIRCLENAME        TEXT,
                CIRCLECD          TEXT
            )
            """
        )
        conn.executemany(
            """
            INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (
                :BRCD,:CUST_NBR,:ACCT_NBR,:ACCT_BRNCH,:AGE_YRS,
                :SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG,:KYC_FLG,:END_OF_DAY_BAL,:MTRTY_DT,

            )
            """,
            [
                {

                    "ACCT_BRNCH": "001",

                    "CIRCLECD": "THN",
                },

@pytest.fixture
def mock_model_service() -> MagicMock:
    """
    Return a MagicMock configured to behave like a loaded ModelLoaderService.

    The mock's ``generate()`` returns a valid SQL response by default.
    """
    svc = MagicMock()
    svc.is_loaded.return_value = True
    svc.generate.return_value = (
        "<begin_sql_response>\n"
        "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1;\n"
        "<end_sql_response>"
    )
    svc.extract_sql.return_value = "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1"
    return svc

"""
tests/test_compliance_processor.py — unit tests for ComplianceProcessor.
"""

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from genai.core.processor import ComplianceProcessor
from genai.core.schemas import ComplianceChatRequest, ComplianceChatResponse, ComplianceOrchestrationResponse
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def mock_graph():
    """A mock compiled LangGraph that returns a successful final state."""
    graph = MagicMock()
    graph.invoke.return_value = AgentState(  # type: ignore
        question="test",
        question_id="qid-001",
        interpreted_question="test?",
        intent_ok=True,
        clarification_needed=None,
        sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
        db_path="data/comp_db/COMP_REPORTS.db",
        sql_review_passed=True,
        review_feedback=None,
        retry_count=0,
        sqlglot_valid=True,
        sqlglot_error=None,
        result_rows=[{"BRCD": "001", "AGE_YRS": 65}],
        result_columns=["BRCD", "AGE_YRS"],
        result_summary="Query returned 1 record(s).",
        error=None,
        stages=["intent_agent:ok", "schema_agent:db=COMP_REPORTS.db", "executor_agent:ok rows=1"],
    )
    return graph


@pytest.fixture
def processor(mock_graph) -> ComplianceProcessor:
    return ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=mock_graph)


class TestProcessQuery:

    def test_returns_chat_response(self, processor):
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Show senior citizens.", question_id="qid-001")
        result = processor.process_query(request)
        assert isinstance(result, ComplianceChatResponse)
        assert result.sql_query == "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL"
        assert result.error is None

    def test_returns_orchestration_response(self, processor):
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Show senior citizens.", question_id="qid-001")
        result = processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        assert isinstance(result, ComplianceOrchestrationResponse)
        assert len(result.stages) > 0

    def test_empty_question_returns_error(self, processor):
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question_id="qid-empty")
        result = processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        assert result.error is not None

    def test_result_rows_in_response(self, processor):
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Show senior citizens.", question_id="qid-001")
        result = processor.process_query(request)
        assert result.df_output is not None
        assert len(result.df_output) == 1

    def test_result_columns_in_response(self, processor):
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Show senior citizens.", question_id="qid-001")
        result = processor.process_query(request)
        assert "BRCD" in result.result_columns


class TestProcessQueryClarificationPath:

    def test_clarification_is_surfaced(self):
        graph = MagicMock()
        graph.invoke.return_value = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            question="give me data",
            question_id="qid-002",
            intent_ok=False,
            clarification_needed="Please ask a specific compliance question.",
            stages=["intent_agent:clarification_required", "clarify_node:terminal"],
            error=None,
        )
        processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=graph)
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="give me data", question_id="qid-002")
        result = processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        assert result.needs_clarification is True
        assert result.clarification_question is not None


class TestProcessQueryErrorPath:

    def test_graph_exception_returns_error_response(self):
        graph = MagicMock()
        graph.invoke.side_effect = RuntimeError("Unexpected boom")
        processor = ComplianceProcessor(compiled_graph=graph)
        request = ComplianceChatRequest(question="Show KYC N accounts.", question_id="qid-003")
        result = processor.process_query_with_orchestration(request)
        assert result.error is not None
        assert "Unexpected" in result.error

FILE: tests\test_db_registry.py

"""
tests/test_db_registry.py — unit tests for the db_registry utility.
"""

import pytest

from genai.utils.registry import (
    get_all_tables,
    get_few_shots,
    get_table_meta,
    list_databases,
    reload,
)


class TestDbRegistryLoaded:
    """
    The registry should load from genai/config/databases.toml automatically.
    """

    def test_get_all_tables_returns_list(self):
        tables = get_all_tables()
        assert isinstance(tables, list)

    def test_comp_tbl_is_registered(self):
        assert "COMP_TBL" in get_all_tables()

    def test_list_databases_returns_list(self):
        dbs = list_databases()
        assert isinstance(dbs, list)
        assert len(dbs) > 0

    def test_comp_reports_db_registered(self):
        assert "COMP_REPORTS" in list_databases()

    def test_get_table_meta_returns_dict(self):
        meta = get_table_meta("COMP_TBL")
        assert isinstance(meta, dict)

    def test_table_meta_has_description(self):
        meta = get_table_meta("COMP_TBL")
        assert "description" in meta
        assert meta["description"]

    def test_table_meta_has_columns(self):
        meta = get_table_meta("COMP_TBL")
        cols = meta.get("columns", {})
        assert "KYC_FLG" in cols
        assert "AGE_YRS" in cols
        assert "SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG" in cols

    def test_table_meta_has_db_file(self):
        meta = get_table_meta("COMP_TBL")
        assert "db_file" in meta
        assert "COMP_REPORTS" in meta["db_file"]

    def test_get_few_shots_returns_string(self):
        examples = get_few_shots("COMP_TBL")
        assert isinstance(examples, str)
        assert len(examples) > 0

    def test_few_shots_contain_examples(self):
        examples = get_few_shots("COMP_TBL")
        assert "SELECT" in examples
        assert "KYC_FLG" in examples or "SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG" in examples

    def test_unknown_table_returns_empty_dict(self):
        meta = get_table_meta("NONEXISTENT_TABLE_XYZ")
        assert meta == {}

    def test_unknown_table_few_shots_empty_string(self):
        examples = get_few_shots("NONEXISTENT_TABLE_XYZ")
        assert examples == ""


class TestDbRegistryReload:

    def test_reload_clears_cache(self):
        """Calling reload() should not raise and should allow a fresh load."""
        reload()
        # After reload the registry rebuilds lazily on next access.
        tables = get_all_tables()
        assert "COMP_TBL" in tables

"""
tests/test_executor_agent.py — unit tests for the Executor Agent node.
"""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.executor_agent import ExecutorAgent, _ensure_limit  # noqa: F401
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def agent() -> ExecutorAgent:
    return ExecutorAgent()


class TestExecutorSelectQueries:
    """Valid SELECT statements should return rows and a summary."""

    def test_select_all_returns_rows(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error") is None
        assert isinstance(result["result_rows"], list)
        assert len(result["result_rows"]) > 0
        assert "result_columns" in result
        assert "result_summary" in result

    def test_aggregate_returns_single_row(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM COMP_TBL",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error") is None
        assert len(result["result_rows"]) == 1
        assert "cnt" in result["result_rows"][0]

    def test_where_clause_filters_correctly(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        rows = result["result_rows"]
        assert all(r["SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG"] == 1 for r in rows)

    def test_kyc_n_filter(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        rows = result["result_rows"]
        assert all(r["KYC_FLG"] == "N" for r in rows)


class TestExecutorErrorCases:
    """Edge cases and error conditions."""

    def test_empty_sql_returns_error(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="", db_path=str(tmp_db))  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error")

    def test_missing_db_path_returns_error(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(sql_query="SELECT 1", db_path="")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error")

    def test_nonexistent_db_returns_error(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT 1",
            db_path="/nonexistent/path/db.db",
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error")

    def test_invalid_sql_returns_error(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE = BROKEN",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("error")


class TestEnsureLimit:
    """Unit tests for the _ensure_limit helper function."""

    def test_adds_limit_when_absent_if_enforced(self):
        """
        When enforce_default_limit=true, LIMIT should be injected.
        When enforce_default_limit=false (current query.toml default),
        _ensure_limit is a no-op — we test only that the return type is correct.
        """
        from genai.nl2sql_agent.executor_agent import _ENFORCE_LIMIT
        sql, injected = _ensure_limit("SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL")
        if _ENFORCE_LIMIT:
            assert injected is True
            assert "LIMIT" in sql.upper()
        else:
            # No limit injected when enforcement is off — this is correct.
            assert injected is False

    def test_does_not_add_limit_when_present(self):
        sql, injected = _ensure_limit("SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL LIMIT 5")
        assert injected is False
        assert sql.upper().count("LIMIT") >= 1  # original LIMIT preserved

    def test_limit_case_insensitive(self):
        sql, injected = _ensure_limit("SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL limit 20")
        assert injected is False

"""
tests/test_fastapi_app.py — integration tests for the FastAPI application.

Uses the ASGI test client from httpx (starlette TestClient under the hood)
to exercise the HTTP layer without starting a real server.

Note: The /api/v1/compliance/* endpoints require a loaded LLM provider
which is mocked here to keep the tests fast and offline.
"""

import pytest
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from application.main import app


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    """Return an ASGI test client for the FastAPI application."""
    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as c:
        yield c


class TestHealthEndpoint:

    def test_health_returns_200(self, client):
        response = client.get("/health")
        assert response.status_code == 200

    def test_health_body(self, client):
        data = response = client.get("/health").json()
        assert data["status"] == "ok"
        assert "version" in data


class TestComplianceChatEndpoint:

    def test_503_when_processor_not_initialised(self, client):
        """
        When the model service fails to load at startup the processor is None;
        the endpoint must return 503.
        """
        import application.main as main_module
        original = main_module._compliance_processor
        main_module._compliance_processor = None
        try:
            response = client.post(
                "/api/v1/compliance/chat",
                json={"question": "How many KYC N accounts?"},
            )
            assert response.status_code == 503
        finally:
            main_module._compliance_processor = original

    def test_chat_returns_200_with_mock_processor(self, client):
        """When a processor is injected, the endpoint must return 200."""
        import application.main as main_module

        mock_processor = MagicMock()
        mock_processor.process_query.return_value = MagicMock(
            question_id="q1",
            sql_query="SELECT 1",
            df_output=[],
            result_columns=[],
            result_summary="0 rows.",
            error=None,
            model_dump=lambda: {
                "question_id": "q1",
                "sql_query": "SELECT 1",
                "df_output": [],
                "result_columns": [],
                "result_summary": "0 rows.",
                "error": None,
            },
        )

        original = main_module._compliance_processor
        main_module._compliance_processor = mock_processor
        try:
            response = client.post(
                "/api/v1/compliance/chat",
                json={"question": "Show all accounts.", "question_id": "q1"},
            )
            assert response.status_code == 200
        finally:
            main_module._compliance_processor = original


class TestOrchestrationEndpoint:

    def test_503_when_processor_not_initialised(self, client):
        import application.main as main_module
        original = main_module._compliance_processor
        main_module._compliance_processor = None
        try:
            response = client.post(
                "/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate",
                json={"question": "Show KYC N accounts."},
            )
            assert response.status_code == 503
        finally:
            main_module._compliance_processor = original


class TestOpenAPISchema:

    def test_openapi_schema_accessible(self, client):
        response = client.get("/openapi.json")
        assert response.status_code == 200
        schema = response.json()
        assert "paths" in schema
        assert "/health" in schema["paths"]
        assert "/api/v1/compliance/chat" in schema["paths"]
        assert "/api/v1/compliance/orchestrate" in schema["paths"]

"""
tests/test_intent_agent.py — unit tests for the Intent Agent node.
"""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.intent_checker import IntentChecker
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def agent() -> IntentChecker:
    return IntentChecker()


class TestIntentAgentClearIntent:
    """Questions with clear compliance domain intent should pass."""

    def test_kyc_question_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="How many accounts have KYC flag N?")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True
        assert result.get("clarification_needed") is None

    def test_senior_citizen_question_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="Show all senior citizen accounts in Mumbai circle.")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True

    def test_balance_question_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="List accounts with balance above 100000.")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True

    def test_maturity_question_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            question="What is the maturity date for senior citizen accounts in Thane branch?"
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True

    def test_count_query_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="How many accounts are in the Thane branch?")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is True


class TestIntentAgentEmptyQuestion:
    """Empty or blank questions should be rejected."""

    def test_empty_string(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert result["clarification_needed"]

    def test_whitespace_only(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="   ")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False


class TestIntentAgentDisallowedActions:
    """Questions with destructive SQL keywords must be blocked."""

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("word", ["delete", "drop", "insert", "update", "truncate"])
    def test_disallowed_keyword_rejected(self, agent, word):
        state = AgentState(question=f"Please {word} all records from the table.")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False
        assert word.upper() in (result.get("clarification_needed") or "")


class TestIntentAgentVagueQuestions:
    """Vague / ambiguous questions should request clarification."""

    def test_pure_vague_phrase(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="give me data")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False

    def test_too_short(self, agent):
        # "show me it" — "show" triggers has_metric via _METRIC_TERMS check
        # so IntentAgent passes it through. Use a truly ambiguous/empty phrase.
        state = AgentState(question="something")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        # "something" is in _AMBIGUOUS_TOKENS; has no metric term; 1 meaningful token
        # → should request clarification (intent_ok=False)
        assert result["intent_ok"] is False


class TestIntentAgentNormalisation:
    """Normalised question should have a trailing question mark."""

    def test_adds_question_mark(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="List KYC flag N accounts")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        interp = result.get("interpreted_question", "")
        assert interp.endswith("?")

    def test_strips_trailing_dot(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="Show senior citizens.")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        interp = result.get("interpreted_question", "")
        assert not interp.endswith(".")

"""
tests/test_schema_agent.py — unit tests for the Schema Agent node.
"""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.schema_checker import SchemaChecker
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def agent(tmp_db, tmp_path) -> SchemaChecker:
    return SchemaChecker(data_root=tmp_path, default_db_path=tmp_db)


class TestSchemaAgentContextBuild:
    """The schema agent should produce a valid schema context string."""

    def test_returns_schema_context(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="Show senior citizens in Mumbai")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        ctx = result.get("schema_context", "")
        assert "COMP_TBL" in ctx

    def test_db_path_set_in_state(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(question="Show all accounts")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("db_path") == str(tmp_db)

    def test_stage_tag_added(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="List KYC N accounts")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        stages = result.get("stages", [])
        assert any("schema_checker" in s for s in stages)

    def test_column_names_in_context(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="How many accounts are KYC N?")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        ctx = result.get("schema_context", "")
        assert "KYC_FLG" in ctx
        assert "AGE_YRS" in ctx

    def test_sample_rows_in_context(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(question="List all accounts")  # type: ignore
        result = agent.run(state)
        ctx = result.get("schema_context", "")
        assert "Sample rows" in ctx

"""
tests/test_schemas.py — unit tests for Pydantic request/response schemas.
"""

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from genai.core.schemas import (
    ComplianceChatRequest,
    ComplianceChatResponse,
    ComplianceOrchestrationResponse,
    HealthResponse,
    LLMConnectionRequest,
    LLMConnectionResponse,
)


class TestComplianceChatRequest:

    def test_valid_request(self):
        req = ComplianceChatRequest(question="How many KYC N accounts?", question_id="req-001")
        assert req.question == "How many KYC N accounts?"
        assert req.question_id == "req-001"

    def test_blank_question_raises(self):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            ComplianceChatRequest(question="   ")

    def test_optional_fields_default_none(self):
        req = ComplianceChatRequest()
        assert req.question is None
        assert req.question_id is None

    def test_question_is_stripped(self):
        req = ComplianceChatRequest(question="  hello  ")
        assert req.question == "hello"


class TestComplianceChatResponse:

    def test_minimal_response(self):
        resp = ComplianceChatResponse(question_id="q1")
        assert resp.question_id == "q1"
        assert resp.sql_query is None
        assert resp.df_output is None
        assert resp.error is None

    def test_full_response(self):
        resp = ComplianceChatResponse(
            question_id="q1",
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
            df_output=[{"BRCD": "001", "AGE_YRS": 65}],
            result_columns=["BRCD", "AGE_YRS"],
            result_summary="1 record returned.",
            error=None,
        )
        assert len(resp.df_output) == 1
        assert "BRCD" in resp.result_columns


class TestComplianceOrchestrationResponse:

    def test_defaults(self):
        resp = ComplianceOrchestrationResponse()
        assert resp.needs_clarification is False
        assert resp.stages == []
        assert resp.result_columns == []

    def test_with_stages(self):
        resp = ComplianceOrchestrationResponse(stages=["intent_agent:ok", "executor_agent:ok rows=5"])
        assert len(resp.stages) == 2


class TestLLMConnectionRequest:

    def test_requires_provider(self):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            LLMConnectionRequest()  # provider is required

    def test_valid_request(self):
        req = LLMConnectionRequest(provider="dlh")
        assert req.provider == "dlh"
        assert req.dlh_verify_ssl is False


class TestHealthResponse:

    def test_defaults(self):
        resp = HealthResponse()
        assert resp.status == "ok"
        assert resp.version == "2.0.0"

"""
tests/test_sql_review_agent.py — unit tests for the SQL Review Agent node.
"""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_reviewer import SQLReviewer
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def agent() -> SQLReviewer:
    return SQLReviewer(max_retries=3)


class TestSQLReviewPassCases:
    """Valid SELECT queries should pass all review checks."""

    def test_simple_select_passes(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True
        assert result.get("review_feedback") is None

    def test_select_with_where_passes(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True

    def test_aggregate_passes(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is True


class TestSQLReviewDMLBlocked:
    """DML / DDL keywords must always be rejected."""

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("kw", ["DELETE", "DROP", "INSERT", "UPDATE", "TRUNCATE"])
    def test_disallowed_keyword_rejected(self, agent, tmp_db, kw):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query=f"{kw} FROM COMP_TBL",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert result["retry_count"] == 1


class TestSQLReviewStructure:
    """Structural checks: must start with SELECT and contain FROM."""

    def test_missing_from_rejected(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT 1",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False

    def test_no_select_rejected(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="WITH cte AS (SELECT 1) SHOW cte",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False

    def test_empty_sql_rejected(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False


class TestSQLReviewMaxRetries:
    """When retry ceiling is reached, error is set and review fails."""

    def test_max_retries_exceeded(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=3,  # already at max
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert result.get("error")


class TestSQLReviewUnknownTable:
    """Queries referencing non-existent tables must be flagged."""

    def test_unknown_table_rejected(self, agent, tmp_db):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM NON_EXISTENT_TABLE",
            db_path=str(tmp_db),
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result["sql_review_passed"] is False
        assert "NON_EXISTENT_TABLE" in (result.get("review_feedback") or "")



####################################################################################################
FILE: tests\test_sql_validator.py
####################################################################################################

"""
tests/test_sql_validator.py — unit tests for the SQL Validator Agent node.
"""

import pytest

from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_validator import SQLValidator
from genai.nl2sql_agent.state import AgentState


@pytest.fixture
def agent() -> SQLValidator:
    return SQLValidator(dialect="sqlite")


class TestSQLValidatorPassCases:
    """Valid SELECT statements should pass AST validation."""

    def test_simple_select_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL WHERE KYC_FLG = 'N'",
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("sqlglot_valid") is True
        assert result.get("sqlglot_error") is None

    def test_aggregate_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT COUNT(*) AS record_count FROM COMP_TBL WHERE SR_CTZN_ACCT_FLG = 1",
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("sqlglot_valid") is True

    def test_order_by_passes(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL ORDER BY END_OF_DAY_BAL DESC LIMIT 10",
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("sqlglot_valid") is True


class TestSQLValidatorFailCases:
    """Invalid or non-SELECT statements should fail validation."""

    def test_parse_error_fails(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="SELECT * FROM WHERE = BROKEN",
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        # sqlglot may fail or succeed on partially valid SQL; if it fails, validate the result shape
        if result.get("sqlglot_valid") is False:
            assert result.get("sqlglot_error")

    def test_insert_statement_fails(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1, 2)",
            retry_count=0,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        assert result.get("sqlglot_valid") is False


class TestSQLValidatorRetryIncrement:
    """On failure, retry_count must be incremented."""

    def test_retry_incremented_on_failure(self, agent):
        state = AgentState(  # type: ignore
            sql_query="INSERT INTO COMP_TBL VALUES (1)",
            retry_count=1,
        )
        result = agent.run(state)
        if result.get("sqlglot_valid") is False:
            assert result.get("retry_count", 0) > 1

"""
tests/test_utils.py — unit tests for utility helpers.

Covers:
- genai.utils.common
- genai.utils.json_utils
- genai.utils.file_utils
- genai.utils.db_utils
- genai.utils.query_optimizer (via nl2sql_agent)
- genai.nl2sql_agent.result_formatter
"""

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from genai.utils.helpers import (
    flatten_list,
    normalise_whitespace,
    safe_json_loads,
    truncate,
    ensure_directory,
    file_size,
    list_files,
    read_text,
    resolve_project_path,
    write_text,
    extract_json_object,
    extract_json_from_text,
    is_valid_json,
    pretty_print,
    safe_dumps,
    safe_loads,
)
from genai.utils.registry import (
    fetch_all,
    fetch_scalar,
    get_column_names,
    list_tables,
    row_count,
    table_exists,
)
from genai.nl2sql_agent.sql_writer_agent import optimise, strip_comments, strip_trailing_semicolon
from genai.nl2sql_agent.executor_agent import (
    extract_scalar,
    format_summary,
    to_csv,
    to_markdown_table,
)


# ── genai.utils.common ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestCommonUtils:

    def test_safe_json_loads_valid(self):
        assert safe_json_loads('{"a": 1}') == {"a": 1}

    def test_safe_json_loads_invalid(self):
        assert safe_json_loads("not json") is None

    def test_truncate_short(self):
        assert truncate("hello", max_len=10) == "hello"

    def test_truncate_long(self):
        result = truncate("hello world", max_len=8)
        assert len(result) <= 8

    def test_normalise_whitespace(self):
        assert normalise_whitespace("  hello   world  ") == "hello world"

    def test_flatten_list(self):
        assert flatten_list([[1, 2], [3], 4]) == [1, 2, 3, 4]


# ── genai.utils.json_utils ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestJsonUtils:

    def test_safe_loads_valid(self):
        assert safe_loads('{"key": "val"}') == {"key": "val"}

    def test_safe_loads_invalid(self):
        assert safe_loads("{bad json}") is None

    def test_safe_dumps_roundtrip(self):
        obj = {"x": [1, 2, 3]}
        assert safe_loads(safe_dumps(obj)) == obj

    def test_extract_json_from_text(self):
        text = 'Here is data: {"answer": 42} done.'
        result = extract_json_from_text(text)
        assert result == {"answer": 42}

    def test_extract_json_object_empty_on_failure(self):
        assert extract_json_object("no json here") == {}

    def test_is_valid_json_true(self):
        assert is_valid_json("[1, 2, 3]") is True

    def test_is_valid_json_false(self):
        assert is_valid_json("bad") is False

    def test_pretty_print(self):
        result = pretty_print({"a": 1})
        assert '"a"' in result


# ── genai.utils.file_utils ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestFileUtils:

    def test_ensure_directory_creates(self, tmp_path):
        new_dir = tmp_path / "sub" / "dir"
        ensure_directory(new_dir)
        assert new_dir.exists() and new_dir.is_dir()

    def test_write_and_read_text(self, tmp_path):
        path = tmp_path / "test.txt"
        write_text(path, "hello")
        assert read_text(path) == "hello"

    def test_file_size_zero_for_missing(self):
        assert file_size("/nonexistent/file.txt") == 0

    def test_list_files(self, tmp_path):
        (tmp_path / "a.txt").write_text("a")
        (tmp_path / "b.txt").write_text("b")
        files = list_files(tmp_path, "*.txt")
        assert len(files) == 2

    def test_resolve_project_path(self):
        path = resolve_project_path("data", "comp_db")
        assert path.is_absolute()
        assert path.name == "comp_db"


# ── genai.utils.db_utils ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TestDbUtils:

    def test_list_tables(self, tmp_db):
        tables = list_tables(tmp_db)
        assert "COMP_TBL" in tables

    def test_table_exists_true(self, tmp_db):
        assert table_exists(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL") is True

    def test_table_exists_false(self, tmp_db):
        assert table_exists(tmp_db, "NONEXISTENT") is False

    def test_fetch_all_returns_dicts(self, tmp_db):
        rows = fetch_all(tmp_db, "SELECT * FROM COMP_TBL")
        assert isinstance(rows, list)
        assert all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in rows)

    def test_fetch_scalar(self, tmp_db):
        count = fetch_scalar(tmp_db, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM COMP_TBL")
        assert isinstance(count, int)
        assert count == 5

    def test_get_column_names(self, tmp_db):
        cols = get_column_names(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL")
        assert "KYC_FLG" in cols
        assert "AGE_YRS" in cols

    def test_row_count(self, tmp_db):
        assert row_count(tmp_db, "COMP_TBL") == 5


# ── genai.nl2sql_agent.query_optimizer ───────────────────────────────────────

class TestQueryOptimizer:

    def test_strip_inline_comment(self):
        result = strip_comments("SELECT * FROM T -- get all")
        assert "--" not in result

    def test_strip_trailing_semicolon(self):
        assert strip_trailing_semicolon("SELECT 1;") == "SELECT 1"
        assert strip_trailing_semicolon("SELECT 1") == "SELECT 1"

    def test_optimise_combines_all(self):
        raw = "  SELECT *  FROM COMP_TBL  -- comment  ;  "
        result = optimise(raw)
        assert "--" not in result
        assert not result.endswith(";")
        assert "SELECT" in result


# ── genai.nl2sql_agent.result_formatter ──────────────────────────────────────

class TestResultFormatter:

    def test_empty_rows_summary(self):
        msg = format_summary([], [])
        assert "no matching records" in msg

    def test_scalar_summary(self):
        msg = format_summary([{"cnt": 42}], ["cnt"])
        assert "42" in msg

    def test_multi_row_summary(self):
        rows = [{"a": 1}, {"a": 2}, {"a": 3}]
        msg = format_summary(rows, ["a"])
        assert "3 record(s)" in msg

    def test_to_markdown_table(self):
        rows = [{"name": "Alice", "age": 30}, {"name": "Bob", "age": 25}]
        table = to_markdown_table(rows, ["name", "age"])
        assert "Alice" in table
        assert "---" in table

    def test_to_csv_has_header(self):
        rows = [{"col1": "v1", "col2": "v2"}]
        csv_str = to_csv(rows, ["col1", "col2"])
        assert "col1" in csv_str
        assert "v1" in csv_str

    def test_extract_scalar_single_cell(self):
        result = extract_scalar([{"count": 99}])
        assert result == 99

    def test_extract_scalar_returns_none_for_multi_col(self):
        result = extract_scalar([{"a": 1, "b": 2}])
        assert result is None
